+/// A top-level `not exists point[.id]` explicitly makes vacancy observable.
+/// One server lane checks the original rule for every candidate and may report
+/// exhaustion only after the physical range ends. It carries no ordinary
+/// filter because rule probes use wider system visibility.
+// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — exact vacancy is the sole non-uniform exhaustion lane.
+pub(super) fn vacancy(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &BTreeSet<(String, String)>, requests: &mut Requests,
+    read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<MergeExpr> {
+    let range = read.range.as_ref()?;
+    let table = tables.get(&read.table)?;
+    let rule = branches::read_rule(table)?;
+    if read.filter.is_some() || read.index.is_some() || !read.keys.is_empty() || read.matching.is_some()
+        || !read.computed.is_empty() || read.cursor_prefix.is_some()
+        || (table.decl.sync.is_some() && !table.decl.online_only) { return None; }
+    fn alternatives<'a>(value: &'a Expr, out: &mut Vec<&'a Expr>) {
+        match value {
+            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => { alternatives(left, out); alternatives(right, out); }
+            other => out.push(other),
+        }
+    }
+    let mut disjuncts = Vec::new(); alternatives(rule, &mut disjuncts);
+    let admitted = disjuncts.iter().any(|value| {
+        let Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } = value else { return false };
+        let Expr::Exists { read: probe, .. } = value.as_ref() else { return false };
+        if op != "not" || probe.range.is_some() || probe.filter.is_some() || probe.matching.is_some()
+            || !probe.seeks.is_empty() || probe.keys.len() != 1 || intent::column(&probe.keys[0].value) != Some("id") { return false; }
+        let Some(target) = tables.get(&probe.table) else { return false };
+        let Some(columns) = super::intent_lanes::probe_columns(tables, synthesized, table, probe) else { return false };
+        target.indexes.iter().any(|index| target.total_unique(index) && index.columns == columns)
+    });
+    if !admitted { return None; }
+    let mut candidate = requests.clone(); let errors = candidate.error_count();
+    let mut system = read.clone(); candidate.read(tables, &mut system, false);
+    let mut lane = read.clone(); candidate.plan_lane(tables, &mut lane);
+    let mut lanes = Vec::with_capacity(2); let mut order = None;
+    branches::push_membership_lane(&candidate, table, &mut lanes, &mut order, system, SYSTEM_LANE, None, None, None)?;
+    branches::push_membership_lane(&candidate, table, &mut lanes, &mut order, lane, VACANCY_LANE, None, None, None)?;
+    if candidate.error_count() != errors { return None; }
+    *requests = candidate;
+    Some(MergeExpr { view_order: Vec::new(), table: read.table.clone(), matching: None, filter: None,
+        index: String::new(), order: range.order, take: range.take, cursor: range.cursor.clone(), lanes, site: read.site.clone() })
+}

+/// An authored equality may already be the right authorization scope while a
+/// named order index leaves it as a residual filter. Print the two exact edits
+/// together: the compatible declaration and the same query selecting it.
+// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — equality-filter advice is mechanically coherent even when it remains advisory.
+fn equality_filter_index_rewrite(table: &snapback4_core::schema::Table, scan: &snapback4_core::ir::Scan,
+    unpinned: &[String], leading: &[String], declaration: Option<&str>) -> Option<String> {
+    use snapback4_core::ir::{BinaryOp, Expr};
+    fn pins(expr: &Expr, column: &str) -> bool {
+        match expr {
+            Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::And, left, right } => pins(left, column) || pins(right, column),
+            Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Eq, left, right } => field(left, column) && independent(right)
+                || field(right, column) && independent(left),
+            _ => false,
+        }
+    }
+    fn field(expr: &Expr, column: &str) -> bool {
+        matches!(expr, Expr::Field { base, name } if name == column
+            && matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == "$filter"))
+    }
+    fn independent(expr: &Expr) -> bool {
+        match expr {
+            Expr::Field { base, .. } => !matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == "$filter") && independent(base),
+            Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => independent(left) && independent(right),
+            Expr::Unary { value, .. } => independent(value),
+            Expr::Array(values) => values.iter().all(independent),
+            Expr::Object(fields) => fields.values().all(independent),
+            Expr::Builtin { args, .. } => args.iter().all(independent),
+            Expr::Get(_) | Expr::Exists(_) | Expr::Scan(_) | Expr::Merge(_) | Expr::Block { .. } => false,
+            _ => true,
+        }
+    }
+    let filter = scan.filter.as_deref()?;
+    if unpinned.is_empty() || leading.len() > 8 || !unpinned.iter().all(|column| pins(filter, column)) { return None; }
+    let title = |value: &str| { let mut chars = value.chars(); chars.next()
+        .map(|first| first.to_uppercase().collect::<String>() + chars.as_str()).unwrap_or_default() };
+    let stem = leading.iter().filter(|column| column.as_str() != "id").map(|column| title(column)).collect::<String>();
+    if stem.is_empty() { return None; }
+    let mut name = format!("by{stem}");
+    let mut suffix = 2;
+    while table.indexes.contains_key(&name) {
+        name = format!("by{stem}{suffix}"); suffix += 1;
+    }
+    let declaration = declaration?;
+    let old = format!(" by {}", scan.index);
+    if !declaration.contains(&old) { return None; }
+    let rewritten = declaration.replacen(&old, &format!(" by {name}"), 1);
+    Some(format!("on table {} add `by {name}: {}`; use the same index in the rewritten query:\n```quarry\n{rewritten}\n```", scan.table, leading.join(", ")))
+}
+
 fn matching_merge_rewrite(schema: &Schema, scan: &snapback4_core::ir::Scan, table: &snapback4_core::schema::Table) -> Option<String> {
     use snapback4_core::{ir::IndexKeyExpr, schema::{RuleExpr, RuleTerm}};
     if !scan.prefix.iter().any(|key| matches!(key, IndexKeyExpr::WordPrefix(_))) { return None; }

index 198e9455c7..c33f192dd7 100644
--- a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/program/lanes.rs
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/program/lanes.rs
@@ -75,12 +75,24 @@ fn range_width(domain: &A) -> Option<u64> {
 // A lane-dependent predicate needs the eager binding snapshot. Plain row
 // predicates can keep the same head strategy as explicitly written lanes.
 pub(super) fn filter_uses_lane(value: &A, name: &str) -> bool {
+    let read_uses_lane = |read: &ast::ReadExpr| {
+        read.keys.iter().any(|key| filter_uses_lane(&key.value, name))
+            || read.seeks.iter().any(|seek| filter_uses_lane(&seek.value, name))
+            || read.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(|filter| filter_uses_lane(filter, name))
+            || read.matching.as_deref().is_some_and(|matching| filter_uses_lane(matching, name))
+            || read.range.as_ref().and_then(|range| range.cursor.as_deref())
+                .is_some_and(|cursor| filter_uses_lane(cursor, name))
+            || read.computed.values().any(|value| filter_uses_lane(value, name))
+    };
     match value {
         A::Name { name: used, .. } => used == name,
         A::Focal { .. } | A::Literal { .. } => false,
         A::Member { base, .. } | A::Unary { value: base, .. } => filter_uses_lane(base, name),
         A::Binary { left, right, .. } => filter_uses_lane(left, name) || filter_uses_lane(right, name),
         A::Call { args, .. } => args.iter().any(|arg| filter_uses_lane(arg, name)),
+        // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — an independent fixed point guard does
+        // not need a snapshot of the varying membership row.
+        A::Exists { read, .. } | A::Created { read, .. } | A::Read(read) => read_uses_lane(read),
         _ => true,
     }
 }

+// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — older devices refuse PAGE5's validator
+// interpretation instead of executing fixed residual or vacancy lanes as v3.
+#[test]
+fn page5_plans_require_branch_pages_v4_from_the_device() {
+    let vacancy = "table holds:
+  item: designs
+  owner: principal
+  unique item
+  read <- .owner = viewer
+  online only
+table designs:
+  owner: principal
+  read <- not exists holds[item = .id] or .owner = viewer
+  online only
+query available(c: cursor ?):
+  return designs first 1 by .id after c
+";
+    let fixed = "table roles:
+  bucket: int
+  principal: principal
+  unique bucket, principal
+  read <- .principal = viewer
+  online only
+table access:
+  item: items
+  principal: principal
+  unique item, principal
+  read <- .principal = viewer
+  online only
+table items:
+  bucket: int
+  at: int
+  read <- exists roles[.bucket, viewer] and exists access[.id, viewer]
+  online only
+query page(bucket: int, c: cursor ?):
+  return items where .bucket = bucket first 1 by .at after c
+";
+    let product = "table items:
+  owner: principal
+  helper: principal
+  status: text <=10
+  at: int
+  read <- (.owner = viewer or .helper = viewer) and (.status = 'open' or .status = 'active')
+  online only
+query page(c: cursor ?):
+  return items first 1 by .at after c
+";
+    for (name, source) in [("fixed", fixed), ("vacancy", vacancy)] {
+        let (_, backend) = compile(source);
+        assert!(backend.schema.required_capabilities().contains("branch-pages-v4"), "{name}");
+        let required = snapback4_core::device_protocol::compatibility(&backend.schema.public_projection());
+        let mut old = snapback4_core::device_protocol::DeviceEngine::current();
+        old.version = "0.4.28-page4".into();
+        old.capabilities.retain(|capability| capability != "branch-pages-v4");
+        let refusal = snapback4_core::device_protocol::check(Some(&old), &required).unwrap_err();
+        assert_eq!(refusal.code, "E_DEVICE_VERSION", "{name}");
+        assert!(refusal.message.contains("branch-pages-v4"), "{name}: {refusal:?}");
+    }
+    let (_, product_backend) = compile(product);
+    assert!(!product_backend.schema.required_capabilities().contains("branch-pages-v4"),
+        "bounded products reuse the existing branch validator");
+}

+/// PAGE5's three new planner families are generated independently so their
+/// evidence does not depend on the broader widening stream selecting a page.
+// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — bounded products, fixed+varying grants and exact vacancy all receive generated cases.
+pub fn page5(seed: u64, shape: u64) -> Case {
+    let mut case = base(seed);
+    let mut rng = Rng::new(seed ^ 0x5041_4745_355f_454e ^ shape);
+    case.program.body = Body::Read { kind: Read::Page, take: 1 + rng.pick(3) as u32,
+        descending: rng.pick(2) == 0, filter: false, owner: false, project: None };
+    case.program.prelude.clear(); case.steps.clear(); case.program.lets.clear();
+    case.program.order = Some(vec!["id".into()]); case.program.named_page_order = None;
+    case.program.principal_arg = None; case.program.residual = None; case.program.guard = None;
+    case.program.search_term = None; case.program.membership_named_reordered = false;
+    case.program.boolean_grant_normalized = false; case.program.kids = None;
+    case.program.tables[0].effects = None; case.program.tables[0].residency = Residency::Online;
+    let bucket = case.args["bucket"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
+    match shape % 3 {
+        0 => {
+            case.program.tables[0].rule = Rule::ProductScope;
+            case.program.domain = Some("(.owner = viewer or .helper = viewer) and (.status = 'open' or .status = 'active')".into());
+            let items = &mut case.program.tables[0];
+            if !items.columns.iter().any(|column| column.name == "helper") { items.columns.push(column("helper", Type::Principal)); }
+            if !items.columns.iter().any(|column| column.name == "status") { items.columns.push(column("status", Type::Text)); }
+            for (table, row) in &mut case.rows { if table == "items" {
+                row["helper"] = json!(if rng.pick(2) == 0 { "alice" } else { "bob" });
+                row["status"] = json!(["open", "active", "closed"][rng.pick(3) as usize]);
+            }}
+        }
+        1 => {
+            case.program.tables[0].rule = Rule::FixedPerRow;
+            case.program.domain = Some(".bucket = bucket".into());
+            case.program.order = Some(vec!["score".into()]);
+            id_membership(&mut case);
+            let access = case.program.tables.iter_mut().find(|table| table.name == "access").unwrap();
+            access.rule = Rule::Member; access.effects = None; access.residency = Residency::Online;
+            access.expire = None; access.not_before = None; access.caps.clear();
+            let roles = Table { name: "roles".into(), columns: vec![column("bucket", Type::Int), column("principal", Type::Principal)],
+                keys: vec![("byBucketPrincipal".into(), vec!["bucket".into(), "principal".into()], true)], caps: vec![],
+                rule: Rule::Member, residency: Residency::Online, effects: None, expire: None, not_before: None };
+            case.program.tables.push(roles);
+            if rng.pick(4) != 0 { case.rows.push(("roles".into(), json!({"id":format!("role-{seed}"), "bucket":bucket, "principal":case.principal}))); }
+        }
+        _ => {
+            case.program.tables[0].rule = Rule::Vacancy;
+            case.program.domain = Some("true".into());
+            id_membership(&mut case);
+            let access = case.program.tables.iter_mut().find(|table| table.name == "access").unwrap();
+            access.rule = Rule::Member; access.effects = None; access.residency = Residency::Online;
+            access.expire = None; access.not_before = None; access.caps.clear();
+            access.keys.retain(|(name, _, _)| name == "byItem");
+            access.keys.iter_mut().for_each(|(_, columns, unique)| { *columns = vec!["item".into()]; *unique = true; });
+            let mut items = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
+            case.rows.retain(|(table, row)| table != "access" || items.insert(row["item"].to_string()));
+        }
+    }
+    case
+}
+
+pub(crate) fn page5_features(case: &Case) -> Vec<String> {
+    let family = match case.program.tables[0].rule {
+        Rule::ProductScope => Some("product-scope"),
+        Rule::FixedPerRow => Some("fixed-per-row"),
+        Rule::Vacancy => Some("vacancy"),
+        _ => None,
+    };
+    family.map(|name| vec![format!("page5/{name}")]).unwrap_or_default()
+}

+#[test]
+fn page5_generated_families_compile_and_compare_natively() {
+    let mut seen = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
+    for shape in 0..3 {
+        for seed in 0..8 {
+            let case = snapback4_difftest::generate::page5(seed, shape);
+            seen.extend(snapback4_difftest::coverage::features(&case).into_iter().filter(|f| f.starts_with("page5/")));
+            let source = case.program.source();
+            execute::compile(&source).unwrap_or_else(|error| panic!("shape {shape} seed {seed}: {error}\n{source}"));
+            let (verdict, executions) = oracle::repeat(&case, &source).unwrap();
+            assert!(matches!(verdict, Verdict::Pass) && executions >= 2,
+                "shape {shape} seed {seed}: {verdict:?}\n{source}");
+        }
+    }
+    assert_eq!(seen, ["page5/fixed-per-row", "page5/product-scope", "page5/vacancy"].map(String::from).into());
+}

+// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — simulator execution covers the PAGE5 fixed
+// point guard beside a per-row membership and exact vacancy exhaustion.
+#[test]
+fn page5_product_fixed_membership_and_vacancy_publish_complete_pages() {
+    let product = bound("table items:
+  owner: principal
+  helper: principal
+  status: text <=10
+  at: int
+  read <- (.owner = viewer or .helper = viewer) and (.status = 'open' or .status = 'active')
+  online only
+query page():
+  return items first 10 by .at
+");
+    assert!(!product.schema.required_capabilities().contains("branch-pages-v4"));
+    let mut engine = Engine::memory(product.schema, product.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
+    engine.seed([
+        ("items".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("a")), ("owner", id("alice")), ("helper", id("bob")), ("status", Value::String("open".into())), ("at", Value::Int(1))])),
+        ("items".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("b")), ("owner", id("bob")), ("helper", id("alice")), ("status", Value::String("active".into())), ("at", Value::Int(2))])),
+        ("items".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("c")), ("owner", id("alice")), ("helper", id("bob")), ("status", Value::String("closed".into())), ("at", Value::Int(3))])),
+    ]).unwrap();
+    let result = engine.query("page", context("alice", &[])).unwrap();
+    assert!(result.complete && result.next.is_none(), "{result:?}");
+    let ids: Vec<_> = result.data.as_array().unwrap().iter().map(|row| row.field("id").unwrap().clone()).collect();
+    assert_eq!(ids, [id("a"), id("b")]);
+
+    let fixed = bound("table chapters:
+  bucket: int
+  principal: principal
+  unique bucket, principal
+  read <- .principal = viewer
+  online only
+table access:
+  item: items
+  principal: principal
+  unique item, principal
+  read <- .principal = viewer
+  online only
+table items:
+  bucket: int
+  at: int
+  read <- exists chapters[.bucket, viewer] and exists access[.id, viewer]
+  online only
+query page(bucket: int, c: cursor ?):
+  return items where .bucket = bucket first 1 by .at after c
+");
+    assert!(fixed.schema.required_capabilities().contains("branch-pages-v4"));
+    let mut engine = Engine::memory(fixed.schema, fixed.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
+    engine.seed([
+        ("chapters".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("chapter")), ("bucket", Value::Int(1)), ("principal", id("alice"))])),
+        ("access".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("access")), ("item", id("visible")), ("principal", id("alice"))])),
+        ("items".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("visible")), ("bucket", Value::Int(1)), ("at", Value::Int(1))])),
+        ("items".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("hidden")), ("bucket", Value::Int(1)), ("at", Value::Int(2))])),
+    ]).unwrap();
+    let result = engine.query("page", context("alice", &[("bucket", Value::Int(1)), ("c", Value::Null)])).unwrap();
+    assert!(result.complete && result.next.is_none(), "{result:?}");
+    assert_eq!(result.data.as_array().unwrap()[0].field("id").unwrap(), &id("visible"));
+
+    let vacancy = bound("table holds:
+  item: designs
+  owner: principal
+  unique item
+  read <- .owner = viewer
+  online only
+table designs:
+  owner: principal
+  read <- not exists holds[item = .id] or .owner = viewer
+  online only
+query available(c: cursor ?):
+  return designs first 1 by .id after c
+");
+    assert!(vacancy.schema.required_capabilities().contains("branch-pages-v4"));
+    let mut engine = Engine::memory(vacancy.schema, vacancy.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
+    engine.seed([
+        ("holds".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("hold")), ("item", id("held")), ("owner", id("alice"))])),
+        ("designs".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("free")), ("owner", id("alice"))])),
+        ("designs".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("held")), ("owner", id("alice"))])),
+    ]).unwrap();
+    let result = engine.query("available", context("bob", &[("c", Value::Null)])).unwrap();
+    assert!(result.complete && result.next.is_none(), "{result:?}");
+    assert_eq!(result.data.as_array().unwrap()[0].field("id").unwrap(), &id("free"));
+}

diff --git a/snapback4/lean/Quarry/Eval.lean b/snapback4/lean/Quarry/Eval.lean
index 88bb1a8455..f53c348985 100644
--- a/snapback4/lean/Quarry/Eval.lean
+++ b/snapback4/lean/Quarry/Eval.lean
@@ -568,6 +568,35 @@ def laneFits (order : List String) (eqs pins : List (String × Expr)) : Bool :=
 def laneDecided (td : TableDecl) (order : List String) (eqs pins : List (String × Expr)) : Bool :=
   laneFits order eqs pins && decidedPins td ((eqs ++ pins).filter fun p => p.1 != "id" && !order.contains p.1)
 
+/-- PAGE5's fixed half of a fixed-plus-varying grant: after substituting the
+page pins, this is one timeless, required exact point whose ordinary read
+rule discloses the same answer to the caller. -/
+def fixedPointGuard (sch : Schema) (outer : TableDecl)
+    (pins : List (String × Expr)) : Rule → Bool
+  | r@(.probe t point key) => match sch.table? t with
+    | some source =>
+      let columns := key.map (·.1)
+      key.length == point.length
+        && point.eraseDups.length == point.length
+        && point.all columns.contains
+        && (point == ["id"] || source.uniques.contains point)
+        && point.all (fun name => name == "id" || source.cols.any (fun col => col.name == name && !col.nullable))
+        && source.notBefore.isNone && source.expire.isNone
+        && r.usableGuard sch outer pins
+    | none => false
+  | _ => false
+
+/-- PAGE5's varying half has exactly one outer-row key component and otherwise
+uses PAGE's existing readable, total membership witness. -/
+def varyingPointGrant (sch : Schema) (pins : List (String × Expr))
+    (order : List String) : Rule → Bool
+  | .probe t point key => membershipEligible sch t point key
+      && (membershipColumns key).any fun pair =>
+        !pins.any (·.1 == pair.2)
+          && (!order.contains pair.2 || pair.2 == "id" && order == ["id"])
+          && laneFits order [(pair.2, .viewer)] pins
+  | _ => false
+
 /-- Each positive alternative either has a prefix-decided rule, its own
 readable guard, or one exact membership witness with every remaining
 conjunct true by its pins. -/
@@ -593,6 +622,9 @@ def compoundDecided (sch : Schema) (td : TableDecl) (pins : List (String × Expr
                   !ps.any (·.1 == pair.2) && (!order.contains pair.2 || pair.2 == "id" && order == ["id"])
                   && laneFits order ((pair.2, .viewer) :: extra) pins
               | _ => false
+            | [a, b] =>
+                (fixedPointGuard sch td ps a && varyingPointGrant sch ps order b)
+                  || (fixedPointGuard sch td ps b && varyingPointGrant sch ps order a)
             | _ => false)
 
 
@@ -621,6 +653,17 @@ def scopedWitnessDecided (sch : Schema) (td : TableDecl)
               && laneFits order [(pair.2, .viewer)] pins
     | _ => false
 
+/-- PAGE5's explicit vacancy: a top-level negated probe of one required
+exact point by the focal row's intrinsic id. The native lane still checks the
+whole read rule per row; this predicate governs only its exhaustion verdict. -/
+def vacancyDecided (sch : Schema) (td : TableDecl) : Bool :=
+  td.read.disjuncts.any fun r => match r with
+    | .not (.probe t [point] [(key, .col .self "id")]) =>
+        point == key && (sch.table? t).any fun source =>
+          source.uniques.contains [point]
+            && (source.cols.find? (·.name == point)).all (!·.nullable)
+    | _ => false
+
 /-- The pins the single-index planner keeps: the page's equalities and bare
 boolean conjuncts, except `id` and an order column other than the first,
 which stay seeks (`Requests::read`; GPT round 3, finding 3). -/
@@ -705,7 +748,8 @@ def branchDecided (sch : Schema) (t : String) (pins : List (String × Expr))
       | none => false
     let case4 := guards && !reservedMembership && !pinnedMembership && alts.isNone && compoundDecided sch td pins order
     let witnessScope := guards && pins.all (fun p => p.2.total) && scopedWitnessDecided sch td pins fs order
-    (total && (case1 || case2)) || (readFree && (case3 || case4)) || witnessScope
+    let vacancy := guards && pins.isEmpty && fs.isEmpty && vacancyDecided sch td
+    (total && (case1 || case2)) || (readFree && (case3 || case4)) || witnessScope || vacancy
 
 def keyValue (k : Key) : Value := .arr (k.map .s)

 ]
 
+def page5Product : Schema := { tables := [
+  { name := "items", cols := [c "owner", c "helper", c "status", c "at"]
+    read := .and
+      (.or (.eq (self "owner") .viewer) (.eq (self "helper") .viewer))
+      (.or (.eq (self "status") (.lit (T "open"))) (.eq (self "status") (.lit (T "active")))) }] }
+
+def page5FixedPerRow : Schema := { tables := [
+  { name := "roles", cols := [c "bucket", c "principal"], uniques := [["bucket", "principal"]]
+    read := .eq (self "principal") .viewer },
+  { name := "access", cols := [c "item", c "principal"], uniques := [["item", "principal"]]
+    read := .eq (self "principal") .viewer },
+  { name := "items", cols := [c "bucket", c "at"]
+    read := .and
+      (.probe "roles" ["bucket", "principal"] [("bucket", self "bucket"), ("principal", .viewer)])
+      (.probe "access" ["item", "principal"] [("item", self "id"), ("principal", .viewer)]) }] }
+
+def page5Vacancy : Schema := { tables := [
+  { name := "holds", cols := [c "design", c "owner"], uniques := [["design"]], read := .eq (self "owner") .viewer },
+  { name := "designs", cols := [c "owner", c "at"],
+    read := .or (.not (.probe "holds" ["design"] [("design", self "id")])) (.eq (self "owner") .viewer) }] }
+def page5VacancyExamples : List Example := [
+  { name := "page5-bounded-product"
+    source := "LLP 3000.036.001 §18: two principal pins times two status pins retain both predicates in four complete lanes"
+    case := {
+      schema := page5Product
+      store := mkStore [("items", [
+        mkRow "a" [("owner", I "alice"), ("helper", I "bob"), ("status", T "open"), ("at", N 1)],
+        mkRow "b" [("owner", I "bob"), ("helper", I "alice"), ("status", T "active"), ("at", N 2)],
+        mkRow "c" [("owner", I "alice"), ("helper", I "bob"), ("status", T "closed"), ("at", N 3)],
+        mkRow "d" [("owner", I "bob"), ("helper", I "bob"), ("status", T "open"), ("at", N 4)]])]
+      viewer := alice
+      program := query [.ret (.page "items" [] none ["at"] false 10 none)] }
+    tables := []
+    expected := r#"{"kind":"answer","data":[{"id":"a","owner":"alice","helper":"bob","status":"open","at":1},{"id":"b","owner":"bob","helper":"alice","status":"active","at":2}],"complete":true,"next":null}"# },
+  { name := "page5-fixed-plus-per-row"
+    source := "LLP 3000.036.001 §18: one readable fixed point may guard one varying per-row membership lane"
+    case := {
+      schema := page5FixedPerRow
+      store := mkStore [
+        ("roles", [mkRow "r" [("bucket", N 1), ("principal", I "alice")]]),
+        ("access", [mkRow "x" [("item", I "a"), ("principal", I "alice")],
+          mkRow "y" [("item", I "c"), ("principal", I "alice")]]),
+        ("items", [mkRow "a" [("bucket", N 1), ("at", N 1)],
+          mkRow "b" [("bucket", N 1), ("at", N 2)],
+          mkRow "c" [("bucket", N 2), ("at", N 3)]])]
+      viewer := alice
+      program := query [.ret (.page "items" [("bucket", int 1)] none ["at"] false 10 none)] }
+    tables := []
+    expected := r#"{"kind":"answer","data":[{"id":"a","bucket":1,"at":1}],"complete":true,"next":null}"# },
+  { name := "page5-exact-vacancy"
+    source := "LLP 3000.036.001 §18: explicit exact-point vacancy makes private server exhaustion observable"
+    case := {
+      schema := page5Vacancy
+      store := mkStore [("holds", [mkRow "h" [("design", I "held"), ("owner", I "alice")]]),
+        ("designs", [mkRow "free" [("owner", I "alice"), ("at", N 1)],
+          mkRow "held" [("owner", I "alice"), ("at", N 2)]])]
+      viewer := bob
+      program := query [.ret (.page "designs" [] none ["at"] false 10 none)] }
+    tables := []
+    expected := r#"{"kind":"answer","data":[{"id":"free","owner":"alice","at":1}],"complete":true,"next":null}"# }
+]

-/// A generated membership lane may retain a second, prefix-fixed point grant
+/// Every sibling of a varying membership witness must already hold. Preserve

+fn fixed_rule_holds<'a>(schema: &Schema, filter: &Expr, rule: &RuleExpr,
+    outer_name: &str, outer_table: &Table, pinned: &impl Fn(&str) -> Option<&'a Expr>,
+    pins: &[(&String, &Expr)]) -> bool {
+    if rule_true(rule, outer_name, outer_table, pinned) { return true; }
+    match rule {
+        RuleExpr::And(parts) => parts.iter().all(|part|
+            fixed_rule_holds(schema, filter, part, outer_name, outer_table, pinned, pins)),
+        RuleExpr::Or(parts) => parts.iter().any(|part|
+            fixed_rule_holds(schema, filter, part, outer_name, outer_table, pinned, pins)),
+        _ => fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, filter, rule, pins),
+    }
+}
+
+/// A generated membership lane may retain prefix-fixed point grants
 /// as a residual predicate.  Recognize only the exact bound point expression:
 /// this proves that every row emitted by the lane satisfies that conjunct,
 /// while a missing, reordered or merely similar probe keeps the merge out.
-// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — one fixed grant may guard one varying per-row grant.
+// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — fixed grants may guard one varying per-row grant.
 fn fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema: &Schema, filter: &Expr, rule: &RuleExpr,
     pins: &[(&String, &Expr)]) -> bool {
     if let Expr::Binary { op: crate::ir::BinaryOp::And, left, right } = filter {
         return fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, left, rule, pins)
             || fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, right, rule, pins);
     }
+    // The IR preserves short-circuit `a and b` as a tiny Boolean block.
+    // Accept only that exact lowering: both operands are necessary, while an
+    // `or` block assigns on the false branch and cannot establish either one.
+    if let Expr::Block { body, value } = filter
+        && let [crate::ir::Stmt::Let { name, value: left }, crate::ir::Stmt::If {
+            condition: Expr::Variable(condition), then_body, else_body }] = body.as_slice()
+        && let [crate::ir::Stmt::Assign { name: assigned, value: right }] = then_body.as_slice()
+        && else_body.is_empty() && condition == name && assigned == name
+        && matches!(value.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(result) if result == name) {
+        return fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, left, rule, pins)
+            || fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, right, rule, pins);
+    }
     let (Expr::Exists(read), RuleExpr::Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. }) = (filter, rule) else { return false };
     let Some(target) = schema.table(table) else { return false };
     let Some(point) = target.index(index) else { return false };
     equalities.is_empty() && target.through.is_none() && target.not_before.is_none() && !target.is_expiring()
         && point.unique && point.is_plain() && point.components.iter().all(|part| part.column()
             .and_then(|column| target.columns.get(column)).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()))
-        && schema.rule_input_is_readable(table, index, key)

         && read.key.iter().zip(key).all(|(expression, term)| rule_term_expression(term, expression, pins))
+        && point_read_is_visible(schema, read)
+}
+
+/// A rule probe uses system authority, but the retained residual point uses
+/// ordinary caller authority. Re-prove that the generated, pin-substituted
+/// point is visible instead of asking about the unsubstituted authored terms.
+fn point_read_is_visible(schema: &Schema, read: &crate::ir::Read) -> bool {
+    let Some(table) = schema.table(&read.table) else { return false };
+    let Some(index) = table.index(&read.index) else { return false };
+    if !index.is_plain() || index.components.len() != read.key.len() { return false; }
+    let pinned = |name: &str| index.components.iter().zip(&read.key).find_map(|(part, value)|
+        (part.column().is_some_and(|column| column == name)).then_some(value));
+    visibility(&read.table, table, &table.rules.read, &pinned).decided_true
 }

+
+/// A membership PAGE with two independent `or` filters stays on the ordinary
+/// incomplete scan. The native membership planner distributes at most one
+/// such filter; the independent model must not silently discard the second.
+// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — undistributed query alternatives are outside the constructive PAGE fragment.
+pub fn page_membership_filter_fallback(seed: u64) -> Case {
+    let mut case = base(seed);
+    case.program.tables[0].rule = Rule::OwnerOrWitness;
+    case.program.tables[0].effects = None;
+    case.program.tables[0].residency = Residency::Online;
+    let access = case.program.tables.iter_mut().find(|table| table.name == "access").unwrap();
+    access.rule = Rule::Allow; access.effects = None; access.residency = Residency::Public;
+    access.expire = None; access.not_before = None;
+    case.program.body = Body::Read { kind: Read::Page, take: 1, descending: seed % 2 == 0,
+        filter: false, owner: false, project: None };
+    case.program.domain = Some("(.bucket = bucket or .bucket = 1)".into());
+    case.program.residual = Some("(.score > 0 or .flag = false)".into());
+    case.program.order = Some(vec!["label".into(), "score".into()]);
+    case.program.named_page_order = None; case.program.prelude.clear(); case.program.lets.clear();
+    case.steps.clear(); case.rows.clear();
+    case
+}

 }
+
+#[test]
+fn membership_page_with_two_filter_alternatives_stays_incomplete_in_lean_and_native() {
+    let have_model = model_built();
+    for seed in 0..4 {
+        let case = snapback4_difftest::generate::page_membership_filter_fallback(seed);
+        let source = case.program.source();
+        execute::compile(&source).unwrap_or_else(|error| panic!("seed {seed}: {error}\n{source}"));
+        let mut engine = execute::engine(&case, &source).unwrap();
+        let outcome = execute::execute(&mut engine, &case, "run", &case.args);
+        assert_eq!(outcome.value["complete"], false, "seed {seed}: {outcome:?}\n{source}");
+        let mut report = Report::default(); campaign::check(&case, &mut report, |_| {});
+        assert!(!report.failed(), "seed {seed}: {report:?}\n{source}");
+        if have_model {
+            let checked = snapback4_difftest::lean::compare(&case, &source).unwrap();
+            assert!(matches!(checked.verdict, Verdict::Pass), "seed {seed}: {:?}\n{source}", checked.verdict);
+        }
+    }
+}

   980	Discovery yields document ids, and each dynamic target lane pins `id`. A

   983	the final tie-breaker just as in every other lane. All lanes therefore share
   984	the existing version-4/5 cursor and frontier rules. Before any row is emitted,

   987	unfinished lane without a head/`through`, §6's frontier is absent and the page
   988	is empty, incomplete and has no continuation: no returned row can sort ahead
   989	of an unread point. Only

  1071	## 16. PAGE3: finish the ordinary private-page plans
  1072	
  1073	Commissioned 2026-10-13; implemented locally. Muse and fresh Codex both
  1074	READY in round 5 at `65d505289f`; verbatim reviews and frozen brief are
  1075	`llp/reviews/3000.036-page3.r5.{muse,codex,brief}.md`.
  1076	PAGE2 left 18 notes in 11 of the 50 odd wave-10 final schemas: 12
  1077	party/static-grant pages, three compound rules, two hidden parent sources
  1078	and one pure membership. This amendment extends the existing plan rather
  1079	than asking builders to merge lists themselves. It changes no read authority,
  1080	replicated data or runtime resource ceiling.
  1081	
  1082	**Static grants and whole-group reads.** An owner-or-readable-static-grant
  1083	rule uses the existing party/grant merge on synced union tables too. The
  1084	server evaluates the positive readable grant once; its holder reads the
  1085	original whole range, everyone else the party ranges. An explicit `first` or
  1086	`last` is a page bound, not a table cap: a role holder may page an unbounded
  1087	table, under the usual request meter. An implicit whole-group read is expanded
  1088	only if its original fixed prefix has an applicable stored cap or fully
  1089	pinned total unique, at or below the examined ceiling. That original bound
  1090	applies to the union. A cap on owner does not bound a staff's whole table.
  1091	A fully pinned total unique may supply a one-row lane with the implicit id
  1092	position, even when its physical index has no unpinned component.
  1093	
  1094	**Full union devices.** The same descriptor and scope serve server and device
  1095	cursors. On a device a full union target's grant lane reads its held authorized
  1096	rows, with the ordinary acquisition, coverage and meter checks; it does not
  1097	probe a remote grant. That held range covers every other target lane, so the
  1098	others are not opened. Missing acquisition stays unknown. Online-only and
  1099	single-arm narrowed sync retain their existing grant deferral. This amends
  1100	LLP 3000.036.000.000 §5.3's planner restriction, while preserving its holding
  1101	and acquisition invariants.
  1102	
  1103	**Pure membership.** Remove the syntactic party requirement from §15. A rule
  1104	`exists members[.id, viewer]` or `exists members[.parent, viewer]` has a system
  1105	range and exact membership descriptors, no party descriptors. All source
  1106	readability, unique/cap proofs, total filters, index/key bounds, shared
  1107	positions and discovery/head fences remain. If the source discovery is cut,
  1108	no target rows or cursor are emitted. An empty complete discovery means an
  1109	empty complete page. Point uniqueness still does not cap memberships.
  1110	
  1111	**Positive compound rules.** Distribute positive `and`/`or` into at most seven
  1112	alternatives plus the original system range. Each alternative retains all
  1113	of its row equality conditions. It contains either one exact membership
  1114	witness, or row equality pins and positive row-independent guards. A membership
  1115	conjunct supplies precisely its own authority; all remaining conjuncts must
  1116	be true by the target lane's pins. A pinned membership target instead uses
  1117	a readable page guard, without discovering hidden source rows. No negated
  1118	probe, arithmetic, fallible predicate or second varying source component is
  1119	admitted. Candidates are speculative and fall back when the physical plan
  1120	or bound-IR validator cannot prove the whole admitted shape.
  1121	
  1122	A new guarded-range descriptor evaluates `page_guard_decision` on its own
  1123	pins. A true readable guard opens only that range, not the other alternatives;
  1124	a false guard opens none. It does not cover other lanes. The system range
  1125	covers every lane only for the system. Status/party lanes, discovered parent
  1126	lanes and guarded lanes share one ordered id-tied position and deduplicate
  1127	there, as in §§3–6. Thus if `R = ∨i (Pi ∧ Gi ∧ Mi)` and W is the total page
  1128	predicate, each lane returns exactly `W ∧ Pi ∧ Gi ∧ Mi`; their union is W ∧ R.
  1129	Guard facts and membership sources must be readable, as in §15, so neither
  1130	empty results nor completeness reveal hidden witness rows. Read-meter cuts
  1131	and frontier rules are unchanged; heap exhaustion remains a refusal.
  1132	
  1133	A nullable small enum's `field != null` has a finite positive expansion into
  1134	its declared non-null values. This retains null exclusion exactly; ordinary
  1135	nullable inequalities against other values keep their original scan. The
  1136	wave-10 archive with four species therefore gets four enum ranges plus its
  1137	owner, readable reviewer and system ranges. Compound volunteer-open and
  1138	parent-teacher/status rules preserve each status condition when opening lanes.
  1139	
  1140	**Readable static predicates.** A `where` disjunction may repeat a readable
  1141	static point, such as `.gleaner = viewer or exists coordinators[viewer]`.
  1142	Every point-key expression must be an infallible, read-free name or literal
  1143	(including canonical signed numeric literals); arithmetic, assertions, calls
  1144	and member reads keep the original scan and short circuit. The point must be
  1145	a total unique with fixed terms, readable by those pins or
  1146	its identical self-witness rule, without a lifetime or read-through boundary.
  1147	It remains in the original predicate for every caller, including the system.
  1148	This extension belongs to static party/where branch merges only. Membership
  1149	discovery and compound-rule expansion retain their read-free total filter
  1150	requirement, mirrored separately by model cases 3 and 4. The physical point
  1151	can cut even though its logical predicate is total. The
  1152	branch frontier therefore excludes an interrupted filter candidate; it may
  1153	continue only from an earlier fully evaluated candidate, so retry cannot
  1154	skip the interrupted row. A source its holders cannot read keeps its scan.
  1155	
  1156	A discovered/guard-admitted merge range pays its admission reservation once
  1157	on both runtime hosts, including empty ranges. Device acquired-row trust does
  1158	not add a second prefix-rule reservation to an already admitted lane.
  1159	
  1160	**Compatibility and model.** `branch-pages-v2` fences guarded descriptors,
  1161	held-union merge behavior and fully pinned point-lane admission; it is derived
  1162	from bound plans, serialized as an optional operation fact, and older engines
  1163	refuse it through the ordinary handshake. Existing descriptor bytes remain
  1164	unchanged when none of these new lane types is present. Membership-only plans
  1165	retain the existing membership capability rules. The Lean surface model
  1166	removes its party requirement, and models the admitted positive rule ranges.
  1167	Enums refuse the entire model module with the existing explicit `type-enum`
  1168	reason before any program is declared supported. Compound where-branch
  1169	composition, nullable constant pins and physically nonportable/ambiguous
  1170	compound plans have their own named emitter exclusions. Native static
  1171	predicate keys that are arguments or signed expression nodes use the named
  1172	`page-static-key-expression` model boundary until their separate readability
  1173	classifier is extended; they are unsupported, never counted as oracle passes;
  1174	unsupported fragments remain explicitly named rather than accepted by the
  1175	oracle. Its logical leaf soundness/coverage proof includes pure membership.
  1176	
  1177	**Hidden-source boundary and exact repairs.** PAGE3 does not scan private
  1178	parent rows whose owner cannot necessarily read them. No cap/index alone
  1179	repairs that absence proof. Copy the parent's immutable owner onto the child,
  1180	keep that copy and its parent reference immutable, keep the parent non-expiring,
  1181	and retain the reference with `on delete restrict`, and check the copy against the parent in the child's
  1182	insert rule (and in every trusted seed/backfill). Replace the parent-owner
  1183	read-rule leaf with the copied owner comparison. The copy preserves authority
  1184	only while the parent owner is immutable; a mutable owner needs transactional
  1185	propagation or the original rule. Existing data must be backfilled before
  1186	adoption. The diagnostic names the actual table, reference and owner column
  1187	and supplies exact source edits. It never recommends making parents public.
  1188	
  1189	**PAGE3 qualification and review dispositions (2026-10-14).** Round 1
  1190	repaired null-exempt bounds, parent reference/lifetime preservation, outer
  1191	conjunction guards and capability scope; round 2 repaired fallible static keys,
  1192	block-lowered probe fencing and defensive model bounds. Round 3 named static
  1193	argument/signed-key model exclusions, with zero-oracle Unsupported regressions.
  1194	Round 4 separated readable-static model filters from the read-free gates for
  1195	membership/compound plans, with actual native/model data and completeness
  1196	comparisons. Round 5's two independent reviewers marked the same revision
  1197	READY. No unsupported model case is counted as a pass.
  1198	
  1199	All 50 copied odd wave-10 final schemas compile: 18 notes in 11 schemas
  1200	become 2 in one (both hidden parents). Applying the exact checked copy repair
  1201	to a further copied corpus gives 0 in all 50. Per-shape counts, input/output
  1202	hashes and source edits are in `snapback4/reports/20261013-page3/`. Final
  1203	400-case campaigns have 22,277 native and 12,686 device executions without
  1204	disagreements; Lean's 1,108 executions retain only the three existing C2
  1205	pagination occurrences, with no new signature. Lean audits 59 theorems with
  1206	standard axioms only, runs 74 examples and seven fixtures. Focused language,
  1207	model, simulator and both device-store suites pass.
  1208	
  1209	The clean locked Linux web artifact at `f11a5e72cd` is 2,310,393 B raw /
  1210	704,662 B gzip / 532,356 B brotli; budget 705,162 B = measured gzip + 500 B.
  1211	Family/absence audits and wasm/client builds pass. Later edits affect only
  1212	language/model/tests, preserving these device bytes. No push or publication.
  1213	
  1214	## 17. PAGE4: complete multi-audience pages and exact scopes
  1215	
  1216	Commissioned 2026-10-14 for wave-11 fix #11. This amendment extends the
  1217	existing bounded planner. No read authority, replicated audience, runtime
  1218	ceiling or cursor codec changes. Query unions containing a public status
  1219	are separate from union *sync*: a public sync branch remains excluded.
  1220	
  1221	**Mixed positive alternatives.** Positive distribution also applies to a
  1222	simple disjunction mixing party equality, public/status equality, exact
  1223	membership and readable static grants. Each alternative retains every
  1224	conjunct and the complete original query predicate. Equality lanes,
  1225	guarded ranges and exact discovered membership lanes share the existing
  1226	ordered id-tied merge, with duplicate positions emitted once. At most seven
  1227	alternatives plus the system range fit. Speculative source/target indexes
  1228	are retained only when the bound validator accepts the whole plan.
  1229	
  1230	Named plain page indexes normalize to their declared column order only in
  1231	a speculative merge candidate. A declined candidate keeps its original read.
  1232	An exact owner/member rule whose varying component is necessarily pinned
  1233	can use the existing party/guard merge; alternative-only pins do not qualify.
  1234	
  1235	**Exact where witnesses.** A positive whole `where` conjunct identical to a
  1236	whole read-rule disjunct can define an exact narrower audience: for example
  1237	`posts where exists authors[.id, viewer] first 20 by .at after c`. Discover
  1238	that readable witness under §§14–16's total point and complete-discovery
  1239	proofs. Other audiences cannot contribute rows to W. Every other conjunct
  1240	must be read-free and total. The original predicate remains in admission
  1241	validation; the system descriptor retains the authored witness, because
  1242	system bypasses R but never W. Its prefix also binds the fixed target pins
  1243	into the shared cursor scope. A hidden source keeps the original scan.
  1244	
  1245	**Pinned components of exact witnesses.** A positive alternative may pin
  1246	one component of a multi-column rule probe. Substitute only a necessary
  1247	infallible fixed equality, leaving one varying source component; the core
  1248	validator independently checks the original field term against that exact
  1249	target prefix. Example: `.manager = viewer and exists reporting[report =
  1250	.report, manager = .manager]` discovers reporting rows with manager=viewer,
  1251	then their report values. The other party's alternative inverts the other
  1252	component. Extra named point equalities survive. No arithmetic, missing
  1253	pin, second varying component or hidden source is treated as proof.
  1254	
  1255	**Parent snapshots.** Selecting `s.id` or a stored scalar field of a required
  1256	point-read row image is a fixed lexical value, not a new row read. In a
  1257	standalone page's predicate (returned or assigned), the compiler binds those
  1258	selections once before planning. Optional rows, reference hops, private/maintained fields,
  1259	rebindings and joined paths keep the original predicate. A snapshot does
  1260	not prove that a child's copied band/manager equals its parent's field;
  1261	source repairs must say when their extra pins narrow the authored scope.
  1262	
  1263	**Membership word search.** The existing compact ordinary server search may
  1264	derive its exact two-component total membership audience on an online-only
  1265	table too. Provision the reverse readable source index and scoped words
  1266	index in one checked speculative candidate. Synced targets retain their
  1267	same exact audience requirement. The device program and sync holdings do
  1268	not acquire a predicate; empty needles retain the original browse arm.
  1269	Readability, non-expiring source, portable keys, opening meter refusal,
  1270	word-head merge and original cursor binding remain LLP 3000.028 §3.3's.
  1271	
  1272	**Honest incomplete forms.** A fallback note distinguishes an analyzer
  1273	limitation (the current admitted ranges cannot prove this positive shape)
  1274	from a truly partial read (its range/source cannot establish absence under
  1275	its declared authority). It names the exact complete line when one can be
  1276	verified; scope-narrowing pins say that they narrow the answer. A hidden
  1277	parent retains PAGE3's checked copy/backfill repair. No cap/index-only
  1278	suggestion is offered for an unreadable witness, and no copied relationship
  1279	is assumed from a successful parent read alone.
  1280	
  1281	**Readable negative static points and finite enums.** A negated static
  1282	probe is a page guard only when its selected key is total unique, its source
  1283	is readable at that key and has no maintained-state, read lifetime or read-through boundary.
  1284	Its absence is then an observable authorized fact. A hidden negative probe
  1285	still cannot open a complete range. Required enum inequality against a
  1286	literal expands to its exact remaining variants; optional enum inequality
  1287	against a non-null value stays residual. No varying negative source is
  1288	inverted. This completes the renovation selectionsFor page (its unpaged budget aggregation
  1289	retains an analyzer limitation) without
  1290	turning timed privacy into an unsupported absence claim.
  1291	
  1292	A discovered/guard-admitted merge range pays its admission reservation once
  1293	on both runtime hosts, including empty ranges. Device acquired-row trust does
  1294	not add a second prefix-rule reservation to an already admitted lane.
  1295	
  1296	**Compatibility and model.** Existing descriptor bytes and semantics serve
  1297	mixed unions and exact where witnesses. `branch-pages-v3` fences pinned multi-column witnesses, readable negative
  1298	static guarded/covering ranges and the compiler-owned `RequireUncapped`
  1299	primitive before an older validator or decoder can encounter them. A nullable
  1300	row assertion in a query checks the read meter after evaluating its point;
  1301	a cut raises the internal E_BOUND that the query boundary reports as an
  1302	unavailable incomplete answer, instead of inventing the author's absence error.
  1303	Ordinary absent/hidden rows still use the authored error, and mutation
  1304	assertions retain their transactional policy.
  1305	The independent Lean surface classifier must reproduce positive mixed
  1306	ranges, pinned witness substitution and exact where scope. Static snapshot
  1307	binding is an explicit syntax normalization, like where-guard lowering.
  1308	Word search stays a named unsupported model fragment; it must execute zero
  1309	Lean oracle cases, while native/device tests cover its existing words engine.
  1310	The direct-grant soundness theorem remains positive: negative-grant scalar
  1311	absence/aggregation, fallible-filter fallback and descriptor overflow have the
  1312	explicit `page-negative-grant-fallback` model boundary, including negative
  1313	probes nested in conjunctions and made static by prefix pins, and overflow of
  1314	distributed alternatives inside conjunctions, with zero oracle
  1315	executions. Standard bounded negative-grant pages use the independent range
  1316	classifier and are exercised against the model. No unsupported case is counted
  1317	as a pass. Fresh Muse and Codex reviews both mark semantic revision
  1318	`193f94ea9da7a046f4ce6fe76d7b527e1686ef90` READY. See the
  1319	[verbatim paired reviews and provenance](reviews/3000.036-page4.provenance.md).
  1320	Qualification is recorded separately in the PAGE4 workstream report.
  1321	
  1322	Model fidelity: lexical witness pins use an opaque classifier-only parameter
  1323	term, preserving variable identity instead of converting observed argument
  1324	values to literals or viewer proofs. The oracle rejects that term in authored
  1325	rule JSON; executable authority always evaluates the original rule. Named
  1326	plain orders normalize only within the independent unfiltered mixed-merge
  1327	fragment. Filtered/one-party named candidates outside that fragment (including declined
  1328	native candidates) have the explicit
  1329	`page-named-index-fallback` boundary and execute zero oracle cases. No such excluded candidate is claimed to be a native failure. This
  1330	retains native fallback semantics instead of claiming a complete owner pin
  1331	on an authored fixed index. Both review counterexamples have regressions;
  1332	the generator includes argument-valued pinned pairs.
  1333	
  1334	Delivered-history integration: an acquired device history is an audience in
  1335	its own right under LLP 3000.007 §18.2, even after live membership expires.
  1336	A membership merge on such a target selects its existing whole-range system
  1337	descriptor on the device without changing the actual viewer. It retains the
  1338	authored W, acquisition, lifetime, retention, meters and shared cursor scope;
  1339	only current-source rediscovery is bypassed. Server execution is unchanged.
  1340	`branch-pages-v3` fences this device interpretation. Existing expiry/paged
  1341	snapshot history regressions run on both stores. NARR retains ownership of
  1342	P9's general unbounded-sync/empty-partition narration; this compatibility
  1343	correction arose from PAGE4 named-order planning.
  1344	
  1345	## 18. PAGE5: bounded products, fixed guards and exact vacancies
  1346	
  1347	Commissioned 2026-10-14 for wave-12 fix #12. PAGE5 extends the bounded PAGE
  1348	planner only where every admitted lane still has a constructive exhaustion
  1349	argument. It does not turn arbitrary Boolean authorization into a complete
  1350	query plan.
  1351	
  1352	**Bounded products.** The planner distributes independent positive
  1353	alternatives in the authorization rule across conjunctions before it
  1354	classifies the resulting lanes.
  1355	For example, two exact principal alternatives and three exact status
  1356	alternatives produce six lanes, each retaining both selected predicates and
  1357	the original query predicate. Distribution is all-or-nothing and remains
  1358	inside the existing eight-lane limit, including the system lane. Overflow,
  1359	negative alternatives and a residual second varying witness retain the
  1360	analyzer-limitation fallback.
  1361	
  1362	The same bounded Cartesian construction applies to positive membership PAGE
  1363	query filters. Every resulting branch must contain an eligible equality pin,
  1364	the system descriptor plus every party or membership descriptor must remain
  1365	within eight, and every original predicate stays in its lane. A product that
  1366	pins the membership witness's varying outer field, overflows, or leaves a
  1367	branch without a usable pin stays on the ordinary incomplete scan. The Lean
  1368	classifier constructs every factor before applying those same exclusions; it
  1369	never treats a failed product as an undistributed complete lane or a second
  1370	`or` predicate as absent. A non-alternative equality or required bare Boolean
  1371	shared by every product branch supplies an eligible pin to each branch.
  1372	Repeated party leaves name one physical audience prefix and count once per
  1373	product branch.
  1374	
  1375	**Fixed grants beside one varying grant.** One dynamic membership witness may
  1376	be accompanied by one or more fixed `exists` guards, independent of authored
  1377	conjunction grouping. A fixed guard is admissible only when
  1378	the lane pins every field term in its target key and the target exposes a
  1379	required, total, unique point that is caller-readable after those exact lane
  1380	pins are substituted. The target must have no hidden source, read-through boundary,
  1381	`not before` boundary or expiry. The compiler substitutes the exact lane pin
  1382	into the guard and keeps that complete guard in the generated query filter;
  1383	an already-static point such as `exists roles[viewer]` needs no substitution
  1384	but remains in that filter. The residual may contain three or more fixed
  1385	guards; its exact short-circuit conjunction chain remains necessary in full.
  1386	The core validator independently matches it to the original rule expression
  1387	and lane pin. Thus the generated guard observes every fixed grant that could
  1388	authorize the authored rule. A hidden, timed, non-total or extra-equality
  1389	guard remains incomplete. Two varying grant sources also remain outside this
  1390	fragment.
  1391	
  1392	**Exact vacancy lanes.** The server may invert a top-level
  1393	`not exists holds[.id]` alternative when `holds.id` is a required, unique,
  1394	plain point and the paged table is online-only. Its vacancy lane scans the
  1395	same full ordered outer range as the system lane and privately authorizes each
  1396	candidate against the original rule; it publishes completion only after that
  1397	authorized walk is exhausted. A predicate-meter or authorization-walk cut
  1398	therefore produces an incomplete page. Filtered vacancy reads, synchronized
  1399	outer tables, compound or non-`id` keys, non-unique points and any shortcut
  1400	around the ordinary per-row authorization check keep the fallback. The target
  1401	may itself be hidden or timed: the private server walk evaluates the authored
  1402	rule's system probe for each candidate, then exposes only the authorized outer
  1403	row. This is a vacancy predicate over the outer row, not a claim that a private
  1404	target's absence is globally observable.
  1405	The intrinsic `id` point is always required and unique even though schema
  1406	column and unique lists omit it; other vacancy keys require both declarations.
  1407	
  1408	**Index rewrite note.** When every missing rule field is already fixed by an
  1409	independent exact query equality, the note prints one concrete declaration
  1410	using the actual table and collision-free index name, followed by the complete
  1411	rewritten query using that same index. The rewrite locates the focal read by
  1412	its source-map span, even when an earlier read uses the same index name, and
  1413	the binder recompiles the printed declaration and query together in tests.
  1414	It omits the repair when the added components or emitted label would exceed
  1415	the portable index bounds.
  1416	When those exact equalities do not establish the rule prefix, the note
  1417	does not offer this rewrite.
  1418	
  1419	**Compatibility and model.** `branch-pages-v4` fences the new validator
  1420	interpretations: fixed residual point guards and exact vacancy lanes. A device
  1421	that only understands `branch-pages-v3` must refuse this IR before decoding or
  1422	executing it; bounded products reuse the already validated positive-branch
  1423	lanes, and existing PAGE4 plans keep their prior capability. The Lean surface
  1424	classifier mirrors the three admissions and the private authorization walk.
  1425	Generated differential cases exercise the new lane families on native,
  1426	simulator and device hosts. Forms outside the fragment remain honest analyzer
  1427	limitations rather than completeness claims.

    48	            .is_some_and(|field| !field.nullable || matches!(value, Expr::Name { name, .. } if name == "viewer"));
    49	    }
    50	    let value = if let Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } = expression && op == "not" { value.as_ref() } else { expression };
    51	    let Some(column) = column_of(value) else { return false };
    52	    column != "id" && table.cols.iter().find(|field| field.name == column)
    53	        .is_some_and(|field| !field.nullable && matches!(field.kind, Kind::Bool))
    54	}
    55	
    56	/// The executable predicate classifier currently knows literal/viewer point
    57	/// terms only. Preserve an explicit boundary for admitted native argument
    58	/// and signed-expression keys rather than reporting a model disagreement
    59	/// under an incompletely translated completeness proof.
    60	pub(super) fn static_key_issue(read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<&'static str> {
    61	    fn missing(expr: &Expr) -> bool {
    62	        match expr {
    63	            Expr::Exists { read, .. } => read.keys.iter().all(|key| model_independent(&key.value))
    64	                && read.keys.iter().any(|key|
    65	                !matches!(&key.value, Expr::Literal { .. })
    66	                && !matches!(&key.value, Expr::Name { name, .. } if name == "viewer")),
    67	            Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => missing(left) || missing(right),
    68	            Expr::Unary { value, .. } => missing(value),
    69	            _ => false,
    70	        }
    71	    }
    72	    read.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(missing).then_some("page-static-key-expression")
    73	}
    74	
    75	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#16 — reproduce implicit bounds from surface
    76	// constraints, independently of the planner's selected index or IR.
    77	pub(super) fn implicit_page(module: &LeanModule, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<ReadExpr> {
    78	    if read.range.is_some() || !read.keys.is_empty() || read.index.is_some() { return None; }
    79	    let target = module.tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
    80	    let mut predicates = Vec::new();
    81	    if let Some(filter) = &read.filter { flatten_and(filter, &mut predicates); }
    82	    let pinned: Vec<String> = predicates.iter().filter_map(|predicate| model_equality_column(target, predicate)).collect();
    83	    let cap = target.caps.iter().filter(|(_, by)| by.iter().all(|column| pinned.contains(column)
    84	        && !target.cols.iter().any(|col| &col.name == column && col.on_delete == Some("setNull"))))
    85	        .map(|(max, _)| *max).chain(target.uniques.iter().filter(|key| key.iter().all(|column|
    86	            pinned.contains(column) && (column == "id" || target.cols.iter().any(|col| &col.name == column && !col.nullable))))
    87	            .map(|_| 1)).min()?;
    88	    if cap > 8192 { return None; }
    89	    let mut page = read.clone();
    90	    page.range = Some(ast::RangeSpec { order: Order::Asc, take: cap, cursor: None, site: read.site.clone() });
    91	    Some(page)
    92	}

    94	pub(super) fn membership_physical_issue(module: &LeanModule, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<&'static str> {
    95	    if read.range.is_none() || !read.keys.is_empty() { return None }
    96	    let target = module.tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
    97	    let decl = module.surface_tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
    98	    let rule = decl.rules.iter().find(|rule| rule.effect == Effect::Read && rule.bindings.is_empty()).map(|rule| &rule.value)?;
    99	    let mut leaves = Vec::new();
   100	    flatten_or(rule, &mut leaves);
   101	    let mut parties = Vec::new();
   102	    let mut memberships = Vec::new();
   103	    for leaf in leaves {
   104	        if let Some(column) = party_column(target, leaf) {
   105	            if !parties.iter().any(|party| party == column) { parties.push(column.to_owned()); }
   106	        }
   107	        else if let Some(membership) = membership_physical(module, target, leaf) { memberships.push(membership); }
   108	        else { return None; }
   109	    }
   110	    if memberships.is_empty() { return None }
   111	    if memberships.iter().any(|membership| !membership.source_admissible) {
   112	        return Some("page-membership-source-unique");
   113	    }
   114	    let mut fits = Vec::new();
   115	    for membership in &memberships {
   116	        let Some(table) = module.tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == membership.source) else { return Some("page-membership-key-bound") };
   117	        fits.push(match &membership.index {
   118	            Some(index) => model_selected_index_fit(module, table, index),
   119	            None => model_plain_index_fit(module, table, &membership.columns, false),
   120	        });
   121	    }
   122	    let (system_pins, branches) = match page_pins(target, read) {
   123	        PagePins::Plain(system) => (system.clone(), vec![system]),
   124	        PagePins::Product { system, branches } => (system, branches),
   125	        PagePins::Invalid => return Some("page-membership-filter-product"),
   126	    };
   127	    if 1usize.saturating_add(branches.len().saturating_mul(parties.len().saturating_add(memberships.len()))) > 8 {
   128	        return Some("page-membership-filter-product");
   129	    }
   130	    fits.push(model_plain_index_fit(module, target, &model_lane_columns(system_pins, &read.order_keys), true));
   131	    for branch in branches {
   132	        for prefix in parties.iter().chain(memberships.iter().map(|membership| &membership.target_parent)) {
   133	            let mut pins = branch.clone();
   134	            if !pins.contains(prefix) { pins.push(prefix.clone()); pins.sort(); }
   135	            fits.push(model_plain_index_fit(module, target, &model_lane_columns(pins, &read.order_keys), true));
   136	        }
   137	    }
   138	    if fits.contains(&ModelIndexFit::Ambiguous) { Some("page-membership-index-selection") }
   139	    else if fits.contains(&ModelIndexFit::Nonportable) { Some("page-membership-key-bound") }
   140	    else { None }
   141	}
   142	
   143	/// Compound PAGE3 remains outside the executable model when its physical
   144	/// lanes cannot be independently reconstructed from the surface. Logical
   145	/// distribution is proved separately; an unsupported read is never a pass.
   146	pub(super) fn compound_physical_issue(module: &LeanModule, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<&'static str> {
   147	    if read.range.is_none() || !read.keys.is_empty() { return None; }
   148	    let target = module.tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
   149	    let decl = module.surface_tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
   150	    let rule = decl.rules.iter().find(|rule| rule.effect == Effect::Read)?.value.clone();
   151	    fn compound(e: &Expr) -> bool {
   152	        match e {
   153	            Expr::Binary { op, .. } if op == "and" => true,
   154	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => compound(left) || compound(right),
   155	            _ => false,
   156	        }
   157	    }
   158	    if !compound(&rule) && !matches!(&rule, Expr::Binary { op, .. } if op == "or") { return None; }
   159	    fn ranges(e: &Expr) -> Option<Vec<Vec<&Expr>>> {
   160	        let out = match e {
   161	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => {
   162	                let mut out = ranges(left)?; out.extend(ranges(right)?); out
   163	            }
   164	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "and" => {
   165	                let a = ranges(left)?; let b = ranges(right)?;
   166	                if a.len().saturating_mul(b.len()) > 7 { return None; }
   167	                a.into_iter().flat_map(|a| b.iter().map(move |b| { let mut out = a.clone(); out.extend(b); out })).collect()
   168	            }
   169	            other => vec![vec![other]],
   170	        };
   171	        (out.len() <= 7).then_some(out)
   172	    }
   173	    let mut simple = Vec::new(); flatten_or(&rule, &mut simple);
   174	    // Existing party and exact point-membership classifiers take precedence;
   175	    // their where alternatives already have an independent physical proof.
   176	    if simple.iter().all(|leaf| party_column(target, leaf).is_some() || membership_physical(module, target, leaf).is_some()) { return None; }
   177	    let leaves = ranges(&rule)?;
   178	    let system = match page_pins(target, read) {
   179	        PagePins::Plain(system) => system,
   180	        PagePins::Product { .. } | PagePins::Invalid => return Some("page-compound-where-branches"),
   181	    };
   182	    // The model's compound classifier deliberately keeps the where-branch
   183	    // composition outside its fragment until it models each source prefix.
   184	    let mut fits = vec![model_plain_index_fit(module, target, &model_lane_columns(system.clone(), &read.order_keys), true)];
   185	    for leaf in leaves {
   186	        let mut pins = system.clone();
   187	        let mut predicates = Vec::new();
   188	        if let Some(filter) = &read.filter { flatten_and(filter, &mut predicates); }
   189	        predicates.extend(leaf.iter().copied());
   190	        let fixed: BTreeMap<_, _> = predicates.iter().filter_map(|atom| {
   191	            let Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = atom else { return None };
   192	            if op != "=" { return None; }
   193	            let (column, value) = if let Some(column) = column_of(left) { (column, right.as_ref()) }
   194	                else { (column_of(right)?, left.as_ref()) };
   195	            matches!(value, Expr::Name { .. } | Expr::Literal { .. }).then(|| (column.to_owned(), value.clone()))
   196	        }).collect();
   197	        for atom in &leaf {
   198	            if let Some(column) = model_equality_column(target, atom) {
   199	                if target.cols.iter().any(|col| col.name == column && col.nullable)
   200	                    && party_column(target, atom).is_none() { return Some("page-compound-nullable-pin"); }
   201	                pins.push(column);
   202	            }
   203	        }
   204	        for atom in leaf {
   205	            let mut substituted = atom.clone();
   206	            crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut substituted, &mut |e| {
   207	                if let Some(value) = column_of(e).and_then(|column| fixed.get(column)) { *e = value.clone(); }
   208	            });
   209	            if let Some(membership) = membership_physical(module, target, &substituted) {
   210	                if !pins.contains(&membership.target_parent) && !system.contains(&membership.target_parent) {
   211	                    if !membership.source_admissible { return Some("page-membership-source-unique"); }
   212	                    pins.push(membership.target_parent);
   213	                    let source = module.tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == membership.source)?;
   214	                    fits.push(match &membership.index {
   215	                        Some(index) => model_selected_index_fit(module, source, index),
   216	                        None => model_plain_index_fit(module, source, &membership.columns, false),
   217	                    });
   218	                }
   219	            }
   220	        }
   221	        pins.sort(); pins.dedup();
   222	        fits.push(model_plain_index_fit(module, target, &model_lane_columns(pins, &read.order_keys), true));
   223	    }
   224	    if fits.contains(&ModelIndexFit::Ambiguous) { Some("page-compound-index-selection") }
   225	    else if fits.contains(&ModelIndexFit::Nonportable) { Some("page-compound-key-bound") }
   226	    else { None }
   227	}

   229	
   230	/// The model's direct grant theorem remains positive. Standard bounded
   231	/// negative-grant pages use the independent branch classifier; direct scalar
   232	/// absence/aggregation and fallible-filter fallback stay explicitly outside
   233	/// that executable completeness fragment until its negative-view proof lands.
   234	pub(super) fn negative_grant_issue(module: &LeanModule, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<&'static str> {
   235	    if !read.keys.is_empty() && read.filter.is_none() && read.range.is_none() { return None; }
   236	    fn negative_probe(expr: &Expr) -> bool {
   237	        match expr {
   238	            Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } if op=="not" => matches!(value.as_ref(),Expr::Exists {..}),
   239	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op=="and" || op=="or" => negative_probe(left) || negative_probe(right),
   240	            _=>false,
   241	        }
   242	    }
   243	    let table = module.surface_tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
   244	    let rule = &table.rules.iter().find(|rule| rule.effect == Effect::Read)?.value;
   245	    if !negative_probe(rule) { return None; }
   246	    fn total(expr: &Expr) -> bool {
   247	        match expr {
   248	            Expr::Name { .. } | Expr::Literal { .. } | Expr::Focal { .. } => true,
   249	            Expr::Member { base, .. } => matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Focal { .. }),
   250	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } => matches!(op.as_str(), "=" | "!=" | "<" | "<=" | ">" | ">=" | "and" | "or" | "in") && total(left) && total(right),
   251	            Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } => op == "not" && total(value),
   252	            Expr::Array { items, .. } => items.iter().all(total), _ => false,
   253	        }
   254	    }
   255	    fn distributed_width(expr: &Expr) -> usize {
   256	        match expr {
   257	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op=="or" => distributed_width(left).saturating_add(distributed_width(right)).min(8),
   258	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op=="and" => distributed_width(left).saturating_mul(distributed_width(right)).min(8),
   259	            _=>1,
   260	        }
   261	    }
   262	    (read.range.is_none() || read.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(|filter| !total(filter)) || distributed_width(rule) >= 8)
   263	        .then_some("page-negative-grant-fallback")
   264	}
   265	
   266	/// Named order normalization is speculative in the native compiler. Only
   267	/// the independently modeled unfiltered mixed range fragment normalizes here;
   268	/// filtered or one-party fallback candidates stay named Unsupported.
   269	pub(super) fn named_merge_fragment(module: &LeanModule, read: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
   270	    if read.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(|filter| !matches!(filter, Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Bool(true), .. })) { return false; }
   271	    let Some(target)=module.tables.iter().find(|t|t.name==read.table) else {return false;};
   272	    let Some(rule)=module.surface_tables.iter().find(|t|t.name==read.table).and_then(|t|t.rules.iter().find(|r|r.effect==Effect::Read)).map(|r|&r.value) else {return false;};
   273	    if !matches!(rule,Expr::Binary {op,..} if op=="or") {return false;}
   274	    let mut leaves=Vec::new();flatten_or(rule,&mut leaves);
   275	    leaves.len()<=7 && leaves.iter().any(|leaf|party_column(target,leaf).is_some())
   276	        && leaves.iter().all(|leaf|party_column(target,leaf).is_some() || membership_physical(module,target,leaf).is_some()
   277	            || model_equality_column(target,leaf).is_some())
   278	}

     1	//! A query page whose predicate, or whose table's read rule, is a
     2	//! disjunction of branches each pinning an index prefix that decides the read
     3	//! rule plans as the merge of the branch ranges (LLP 3000.036.001): `tasks where
     4	//! .owner = viewer or .assignee = viewer last 50 by .at after c`, or the same
     5	//! list read through `read <- .owner = viewer or .assignee = viewer`. A page
     6	//! its own pins already decide is never rewritten, so no complete plan, index
     7	//! or cursor changes.
     8	use super::{catalog::TableInfo, intent::{self, Requests}};
     9	use crate::ast::*;
    10	use crate::diag::Site;
    11	use snapback4_core::ir::{BRANCH_LANE, GRANT_LANE, MEMBERSHIP_LANE, SYSTEM_LANE};
    12	use std::collections::BTreeMap;
    13	
    14	/// At most this many branches: each is an index range read ahead by one
    15	/// page, and each may be an index the planner synthesizes.
    16	pub(super) const MAX_BRANCHES: usize = 8;
    17	
    18	type Tables = BTreeMap<String, TableInfo>;
    19	
    20	/// One branch's conjuncts, and whether it is the grant branch.
    21	type Branch = (Vec<Expr>, bool);
    22	
    23	/// Only turn an implicit whole-group read into a page when the original
    24	/// prefix has a stored bound. A party cap does not bound a staff's all-row
    25	/// branch. The original bound applies to the union, rather than each party.
    26	fn bounded_page(tables: &Tables, requests: &Requests, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<ReadExpr> {
    27	    if read.range.is_some() { return Some(read.clone()); }
    28	    let table = tables.get(&read.table)?;
    29	    let mut planned = read.clone();
    30	    let mut probe = requests.clone();
    31	    probe.read(tables, &mut planned, false);
    32	    let pinned: Vec<String> = planned.keys.iter().filter_map(|key| key.column.clone()).collect();
    33	    let cap = table.decl.caps.iter().filter(|cap| cap.by.iter().all(|column| pinned.contains(column))
    34	        && !cap.by.iter().any(|column| table.decl.columns.iter().any(|field| &field.name == column
    35	            && field.on_delete.as_ref().is_some_and(|(action, _)| action == &OnDelete::SetNull))))
    36	        .map(|cap| cap.max).chain(table.indexes.iter().filter(|index| table.total_unique(index))
    37	            .filter(|index| index.columns.iter().all(|column| pinned.contains(column))).map(|_| 1)).min()?;
    38	    if u64::from(cap) > snapback4_core::Ceilings::default().examined { return None; }
    39	    let mut page = read.clone();
    40	    page.range = Some(RangeSpec { order: Order::Asc, take: cap, cursor: None, site: read.site.clone() });
    41	    Some(page)
    42	}
