    44	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#2 — the rule: where-branches first, then the read rule's parties and readable grants.
    45	pub(super) fn expand(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &std::collections::BTreeSet<(String, String)>, requests: &mut Requests, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<MergeExpr> {
    46	    // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#16 — implicit whole-group reads retain their proven schema bound.
    47	    let mut authored = read.clone();
    48	    // A named plain range states the same column order. Normalize only a
    49	    // speculative merge input; a declined candidate retains the authored read.
    50	    if authored.range.is_some() && authored.keys.is_empty() && authored.matching.is_none()
    51	        && authored.computed.is_empty() && authored.cursor_prefix.is_none()
    52	        && let Some(index) = authored.index.as_ref().and_then(|name| tables.get(&authored.table)?.indexes.iter().find(|index| &index.name == name))
    53	        && index.is_plain() {
    54	        authored.order_keys = index.columns.clone(); authored.index = None;
    55	    }
    56	    let normalized = bounded_page(tables, requests, &authored)?;
    57	    let read = &normalized;
    58	    // Vacancy is an exact negative lane, rather than a generic positive
    59	    // grant branch. Classify it before the positive branch planner.
    60	    // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — exact vacancy retains a full private scan.
    61	    if let Some(merge) = super::intent_compound::vacancy(tables, synthesized, requests, read) { return Some(merge); }
    62	    if let Some(merge) = scoped_membership(tables, synthesized, requests, read) { return Some(merge); }
    63	    if let Some(merge) = membership(tables, synthesized, requests, read) { return Some(merge); }
    64	    // An exact owner-or-membership rule belongs wholly to PAGE. If one of its
    65	    // stricter eligibility checks declines, keep the original scan instead of
    66	    // reinterpreting its probe as a generic grant branch.
    67	    if owner_membership_shape(tables, synthesized, read) && !pinned_memberships(tables, synthesized, read) { return None; }
    68	    let range = read.range.as_ref()?;
    69	    let Some(branches) = branches(tables, synthesized, requests, read, true) else {
    70	        return super::intent_compound::expand(tables, synthesized, requests, read);
    71	    };
    72	    let mut candidate = requests.clone();
    73	    let errors = candidate.error_count();
    74	    let mut lanes = Vec::with_capacity(branches.len());
    75	    let mut order: Option<Vec<String>> = None;
    76	    for (conjuncts, grant) in branches {
    77	        let mut lane = read.clone();
    78	        lane.filter = intent::conjunction(conjuncts);
    79	        // The grant lane is the read as written: plan it exactly as the
    80	        // single-index plan would, so the binding check's fallback to it is
    81	        // that plan (an order-column pin a guard needs stays a pin, LLP 3000.036
    82	        // §16.3); its order must still match the party lanes', or no merge.
    83	        if grant { candidate.read(tables, &mut lane, false); } else { candidate.plan_lane(tables, &mut lane); }
    84	        if !grant && !decided(tables, &lane) { return None; }
    85	        // Every lane resumes from one shared position: the same order columns
    86	        // after its pins (lane planning never pins an order column).
    87	        if order.get_or_insert_with(|| lane.order_keys.clone()) != &lane.order_keys || lane.index.is_none() { return None; }
    88	        // An index holds at most 8 components; a lane never makes a program refuse.
    89	        if lane.keys.len() + lane.order_keys.len() > 8
    90	            || !candidate.branch_index_fits(&tables[&read.table], &lane) { return None; }
    91	        lanes.push(MergeLane { admission: read.filter.clone(), nulls: None, distinct: None, branch: Some(if grant { GRANT_LANE } else { BRANCH_LANE }.into()),
    92	            planned: Some(Box::new(lane)), project: None, table: None, prefix: Vec::new(), columns: None, loop_: None, site: read.site.clone() });
    93	    }
    94	    if candidate.error_count() != errors { return None; }
    95	    *requests = candidate;
    96	    Some(MergeExpr { view_order: Vec::new(), table: read.table.clone(), matching: None, filter: None, index: String::new(), order: range.order, take: range.take,
    97	        cursor: range.cursor.clone(), lanes, site: read.site.clone() })
    98	}
    99	
   100	/// An exact positive where witness that is itself a whole read-rule disjunct
   101	/// restricts W to that audience. Enumerate it directly; other audiences cannot
   102	/// contribute rows to W, including the system's rule bypass.
   103	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#17 — author-scoped pages retain their exact witness.
   104	fn scoped_membership(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &std::collections::BTreeSet<(String, String)>, requests: &mut Requests, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<MergeExpr> {
   105	    let table = tables.get(&read.table)?;
   106	    let rule = read_rule(table)?;
   107	    let mut leaves = Vec::new(); disjuncts(rule, &mut leaves);
   108	    let mut predicates = Vec::new(); intent::split(*read.filter.clone()?, &mut predicates);
   109	    for (at, predicate) in predicates.iter().enumerate() {
   110	        if !matches!(predicate, Expr::Exists { .. }) || !leaves.iter().any(|leaf|
   111	            crate::print::expression(leaf) == crate::print::expression(predicate)) { continue; }
   112	        let rest: Vec<_> = predicates.iter().enumerate().filter(|(n, _)| *n != at).map(|(_, p)| p.clone()).collect();
   113	        if !rest.iter().all(total) { continue; }
   114	        let mut narrowed = read.clone(); narrowed.filter = intent::conjunction(rest);
   115	        let mut local = tables.clone();
   116	        local.get_mut(&read.table)?.decl.rules.iter_mut().find(|rule| rule.effect == Effect::Read)?.value = predicate.clone();
   117	        let mut candidate = requests.clone();
   118	        let mut merge = membership(&local, synthesized, &mut candidate, &narrowed)?;
   119	        // The system bypasses R, never W. Its whole-range descriptor retains
   120	        // the authored witness, and also binds all static target pins into
   121	        // the shared cursor scope. Other callers use exact discovery only.
   122	        for lane in &mut merge.lanes {
   123	            if lane.branch.as_deref() == Some(SYSTEM_LANE) {
   124	                let planned = lane.planned.as_mut()?;
   125	                let mut residual = planned.filter.take().map(|p| vec![*p]).unwrap_or_default();
   126	                residual.push(predicate.clone()); planned.filter = intent::conjunction(residual);
   127	            }
   128	            lane.admission = read.filter.clone();
   129	        }
   130	        *requests = candidate;
   131	        return Some(merge);
   132	    }
   133	    None
   134	}

   137	pub(super) fn membership(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &std::collections::BTreeSet<(String, String)>, requests: &mut Requests, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<MergeExpr> {

   141	    if table.maintained.is_some() || table.through.is_some() || table.decl.ephemeral.is_some() || !table.captures.is_empty() || table.decl.public.is_some() { return None; }

   148	    let (mut parties, mut memberships) = (Vec::new(), Vec::new());

   153	    }
   154	    if memberships.is_empty() { return None; }

   156	    // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — an invalid or overflowing product is a
   157	    // declined PAGE candidate, never an undistributed complete membership lane.
   158	    let has_alternatives = conjuncts.iter().any(|conjunct|
   159	        matches!(conjunct, Expr::Binary { op, .. } if op == "or"));

   161	        Some(branches) => branches,
   162	        None if has_alternatives => return None,
   163	        None => vec![conjuncts.clone()],

   166	    // before the candidate can retain party/source indexes the original scan

   169	    if membership_targets.iter().any(|target| base.iter().any(|branch|
   170	        branch.iter().filter_map(|predicate| intent::equality(predicate, table)).any(|(column, _)| column == *target))) { return None; }
   171	    let descriptor_count = 1usize.saturating_add(base.len().saturating_mul(parties.len().saturating_add(memberships.len())));
   172	    if descriptor_count > MAX_BRANCHES { return None; }

   174	    let errors = candidate.error_count();
   175	    let mut lanes = Vec::with_capacity(descriptor_count);

   178	    let mut system = read.clone(); candidate.read(tables, &mut system, false);
   179	    push_membership_lane(&candidate, table, &mut lanes, &mut order, system, SYSTEM_LANE, None, None, read.filter.clone())?;

   201	            // its row may project any membership parent without ordering the

   203	            // as exact residuals on that one row. Discard the provisional
   204	            // inferred index when the authored point supplies this proof.

   207	                && one_row_source_admitted(source_table, source, index))
   208	                .min_by_key(|index| (index.columns.len(), &index.name))
   209	                .map(|index| (index.name.clone(), index.columns.clone()));

   230	                && source_index.columns.get(source.keys.len()) == Some(&source_column);
   231	            let one_row = source.keys.len() == source_index.columns.len() && source_table.total_unique(source_index);
   232	            if !next_component && !one_row { return None; }

   237	                || membership_source_capped(tables, source);

   239	                || !source_read_admitted(source_table, source)
   240	                || candidate.branch_index_width(source_table, source).is_none_or(|width| width > 8)
   241	                || !candidate.branch_index_fits(source_table, source) { return None; }
   242	            if !admitted {
   243	                cap_repairs.push((source.table.clone(), membership_cap_repair(source_table, source, source_index)?));
   244	            }
   245	            push_membership_lane(&candidate, table, &mut lanes, &mut order, *lane.planned.take()?, MEMBERSHIP_LANE,

   251	        cap_repairs.sort(); cap_repairs.dedup();
   252	        let declarations = cap_repairs.iter().map(|(table, declaration)| format!("`{declaration}` on table {table}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" and ");

   255	    }

   261	fn pinned_memberships(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &std::collections::BTreeSet<(String,String)>, read: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
   262	    let Some(table) = tables.get(&read.table) else { return false };
   263	    let Some(rule) = read_rule(table) else { return false };
   264	    let mut conjuncts=Vec::new(); if let Some(filter)=&read.filter {intent::split(*filter.clone(),&mut conjuncts);}
   265	    let pins: Vec<_> = conjuncts.iter().filter_map(|term| intent::equality(term,table))
   266	        .filter(|(column,_)| !read.order_keys.contains(column)).map(|(column,_)|column).collect();
   267	    let mut leaves=Vec::new();disjuncts(rule,&mut leaves);
   268	    let members:Vec<_>=leaves.iter().filter(|leaf|membership_probe_point(tables,synthesized,table,leaf)).collect();
   269	    !members.is_empty() && members.iter().all(|leaf| membership_target(leaf).is_some_and(|target|pins.contains(&target)))
   270	}
   271	
   272	fn source_key_equality(key: KeyPart) -> Option<Expr> {
   273	    let column = key.column?;
   274	    let site = key.site;
   275	    Some(Expr::Binary { op: "=".into(), left: Box::new(Expr::Member {
   276	        base: Box::new(Expr::Focal { site: site.clone() }), name: column, site: site.clone(),
   277	    }), right: Box::new(key.value), site })
   278	}
   279	
   280	fn one_row_source_admitted(table: &TableInfo, source: &ReadExpr, index: &IndexDecl) -> bool {
   281	    let mut point = source.clone();
   282	    point.keys.retain(|key| key.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|column| index.columns.contains(column)));
   283	    source_read_admitted(table, &point)
   284	}
   285	
   286	fn source_read_admitted(table: &TableInfo, source: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
   287	    table.decl.public.is_some() || read_rule(table).is_some_and(|rule| by_form(rule, source))
   288	}

   290	/// Confirm, while requests still live in the throwaway candidate, that every

   294	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#14 — membership candidates commit only after their complete witness is visible in discovery.
   295	fn membership_source_witness(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &std::collections::BTreeSet<(String, String)>,

   300	        || source_fixed(source, &column, &key.value))
   301	}
   302	
   303	fn source_fixed(source: &ReadExpr, column: &str, value: &Expr) -> bool {
   304	    source.keys.iter().any(|key| key.column.as_deref() == Some(column) && same_fixed(&key.value, value))
   305	        || source.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(|filter| required_source_equality(filter, column, value))
   306	}

    11	/// Comparisons and readable static points have a total logical predicate.
    12	/// A physical point can still cut: the merge frontier excludes an unfinished
    13	/// filter candidate, so continuation retries it rather than skipping it.
    14	pub(super) fn total_readable(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &BTreeSet<(String, String)>, focal: &TableInfo, value: &Expr) -> bool {
    15	    if branches::total(value) { return true; }
    16	    match value {
    17	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "and" || op == "or" =>
    18	            total_readable(tables, synthesized, focal, left) && total_readable(tables, synthesized, focal, right),

    20	            if probe.range.is_some() || probe.filter.is_some() || probe.matching.is_some() || !probe.seeks.is_empty() { return false; }
    21	            if !probe.keys.iter().all(|key| fixed_key_total(&key.value)) { return false; }
    22	            let Some(target) = tables.get(&probe.table) else { return false };

    30	        }
    31	        _ => false,
    32	    }
    33	}

    35	/// Distribution must not evaluate a fallible key on an overlapping party's

   164	/// A top-level `not exists point[.id]` explicitly makes vacancy observable.
   165	/// One server lane checks the original rule for every candidate and may report
   166	/// exhaustion only after the physical range ends. It carries no ordinary
   167	/// filter because rule probes use wider system visibility.
   168	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — exact vacancy is the sole non-uniform exhaustion lane.
   169	pub(super) fn vacancy(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &BTreeSet<(String, String)>, requests: &mut Requests,
   170	    read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<MergeExpr> {
   171	    let range = read.range.as_ref()?;
   172	    let table = tables.get(&read.table)?;
   173	    let rule = branches::read_rule(table)?;
   174	    if read.filter.is_some() || read.index.is_some() || !read.keys.is_empty() || read.matching.is_some()
   175	        || !read.computed.is_empty() || read.cursor_prefix.is_some()
   176	        || (table.decl.sync.is_some() && !table.decl.online_only) { return None; }

   178	        match value {
   179	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => { alternatives(left, out); alternatives(right, out); }
   180	            other => out.push(other),
   181	        }
   182	    }
   183	    let mut disjuncts = Vec::new(); alternatives(rule, &mut disjuncts);
   184	    let admitted = disjuncts.iter().any(|value| {

   186	        let Expr::Exists { read: probe, .. } = value.as_ref() else { return false };
   187	        if op != "not" || probe.range.is_some() || probe.filter.is_some() || probe.matching.is_some()
   188	            || !probe.seeks.is_empty() || probe.keys.len() != 1 || intent::column(&probe.keys[0].value) != Some("id") { return false; }
   189	        let Some(target) = tables.get(&probe.table) else { return false };
   190	        let Some(columns) = super::intent_lanes::probe_columns(tables, synthesized, table, probe) else { return false };

   197	    let mut lanes = Vec::with_capacity(2); let mut order = None;

   199	    branches::push_membership_lane(&candidate, table, &mut lanes, &mut order, lane, VACANCY_LANE, None, None, None)?;
   200	    if candidate.error_count() != errors { return None; }

   203	        index: String::new(), order: range.order, take: range.take, cursor: range.cursor.clone(), lanes, site: read.site.clone() })
   204	}
   205	
   206	fn compound(rule: &Expr) -> bool {
   207	    match rule {
   208	        Expr::Binary { op, .. } if op == "and" || op == "!=" => true,
   209	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => compound(left) || compound(right),
   210	        _ => false,
   211	    }
   212	}
   213	
   214	/// At most seven positive alternatives plus the system range. A nullable
   215	/// enum's `!= null` is exactly its finite set of non-null values.
   216	fn dnf(table: &TableInfo, value: &Expr) -> Option<Vec<Vec<Expr>>> {
   217	    let out = match value {
   218	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => {
   219	            let mut out = dnf(table, left)?; out.extend(dnf(table, right)?); out
   220	        }
   221	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "and" => {
   222	            let a = dnf(table, left)?; let b = dnf(table, right)?;
   223	            if a.len().saturating_mul(b.len()) >= branches::MAX_BRANCHES { return None; }
   224	            a.into_iter().flat_map(|a| b.iter().map(move |b| { let mut out = a.clone(); out.extend(b.clone()); out })).collect()
   225	        }
   226	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, site } if op == "!=" => {
   227	            let (column, term) = if let Some(column) = intent::column(left) { (column, right) } else { (intent::column(right)?, left) };
   228	            let kind = table.columns.get(column)?;
   229	            let TypeKind::Enum { variants } = &kind.kind else { return None; };
   230	            let include = |variant: &String| match term.as_ref() {
   231	                Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Null, .. } if kind.optional => Some(true),
   232	                Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Str(excluded), .. } if !kind.optional => Some(variant != excluded),
   233	                _ => None,
   234	            };
   235	            if variants.iter().any(|variant| include(variant).is_none()) { return None; }
   236	            variants.iter().filter(|variant| include(variant).unwrap()).map(|variant| vec![Expr::Binary { op: "=".into(), left: Box::new(Expr::Member {
   237	                base: Box::new(Expr::Focal { site: site.clone() }), name: column.into(), site: site.clone() }),
   238	                right: Box::new(Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Str(variant.clone()), site: site.clone() }), site: site.clone() }]).collect()
   239	        }
   240	        other => vec![vec![other.clone()]],
   241	    };
   242	    (out.len() < branches::MAX_BRANCHES).then_some(out)
   243	}

   100	/// An exact positive where witness that is itself a whole read-rule disjunct
   101	/// restricts W to that audience. Enumerate it directly; other audiences cannot
   102	/// contribute rows to W, including the system's rule bypass.
   103	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#17 — author-scoped pages retain their exact witness.
   104	fn scoped_membership(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &std::collections::BTreeSet<(String, String)>, requests: &mut Requests, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<MergeExpr> {

  1071	/// Whether `e` mentions the focal row.
  1072	fn reads_row(e: &Expr) -> bool {
  1073	    match e {
  1074	        Expr::Focal { .. } => true,
  1075	        Expr::Name { .. } | Expr::Literal { .. } => false,
  1076	        Expr::Member { base, .. } => reads_row(base),
  1077	        Expr::Unary { value, .. } | Expr::Assert { value, .. } => reads_row(value),
  1078	        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => reads_row(left) || reads_row(right),
  1079	        Expr::Call { args, .. } => args.iter().any(reads_row),
  1080	        Expr::Array { items, .. } => items.iter().any(reads_row),
  1081	        Expr::If { then, condition, otherwise, .. } => reads_row(then) || reads_row(condition) || reads_row(otherwise),
  1082	        // Nested reads, objects and the rest: conservatively the filter.
  1083	        _ => true,
  1084	    }
  1085	}
  1086	
  1087	fn flatten_or<'e>(e: &'e Expr, out: &mut Vec<&'e Expr>) {
  1088	    match e {
  1089	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => {
  1090	            flatten_or(left, out);
  1091	            flatten_or(right, out);
  1092	        }
  1093	        other => out.push(other),
  1094	    }
  1095	}

  1107	        Kind::Text(None) => usize::MAX,
  1108	        Kind::Int => 9,

  1129	fn model_declared_indexes<'a>(module: &'a LeanModule, table: &Table, columns: &[String], cursor: bool) -> Vec<&'a ast::IndexDecl> {
  1130	    let Some(decl) = module.surface_tables.iter().find(|decl| decl.name == table.name) else { return Vec::new() };
  1131	    decl.indexes.iter().filter(|index| index.is_plain() && (index.columns == columns

  1136	fn model_index_label_fits(module: &LeanModule, table: &Table, columns: &[String], declared: Option<&ast::IndexDecl>) -> bool {
  1137	    let Some(decl) = module.surface_tables.iter().find(|decl| decl.name == table.name) else { return false };
  1138	    if let Some(index) = declared.or_else(|| decl.indexes.iter().filter(|index| index.is_plain() && index.columns == columns).min_by_key(|index| &index.name)) {
  1139	        return table.name.len().saturating_add(index.name.len()) <= snapback4_core::rows::MAX_INDEX_LABEL_BYTES;
  1140	    }
  1141	    let mut components = columns.to_vec();
  1142	    if components.len() > 1 && components.last().is_some_and(|column| column == "id") { components.pop(); }
  1143	    let base = format!("by{}", components.iter().map(|column| model_title(column)).collect::<String>());
  1144	    // `$intent:{base}:` plus the ordinary 16-byte digest suffix. A collision
  1145	    // can only lengthen the suffix, so a name already over budget cannot be a
  1146	    // physical PAGE lane on every store.
  1147	    let generated = "$intent:".len().saturating_add(base.len()).saturating_add(1 + 16);
  1148	    table.name.len().saturating_add(generated) <= snapback4_core::rows::MAX_INDEX_LABEL_BYTES
  1149	}

  1154	fn model_plain_index_fit(module: &LeanModule, table: &Table, columns: &[String], cursor: bool) -> ModelIndexFit {

  1157	    let selected = declared.first().copied();

  1163	}

  1167	    let Some(index) = decl.indexes.iter().find(|index| index.name == name && index.is_plain()) else { return ModelIndexFit::Nonportable };

  1169	        && model_index_label_fits(module, table, &index.columns, Some(index))
  1170	        && index.columns.iter().fold(PORTABLE_ID_BYTES, |bytes, column| {
  1171	            bytes.saturating_add(table.col(column).map_or(usize::MAX, |kind| model_column_bytes(&kind)))
  1172	        }) <= PORTABLE_KEY_BYTES;
  1173	    if fits { ModelIndexFit::Fits } else { ModelIndexFit::Nonportable }

  1176	fn model_read_by_form(rule: &Expr, pins: &[(String, &Expr)]) -> bool {
  1177	    let pinned = |column: &str| pins.iter().find_map(|(name, value)| (name == column).then_some(*value));
  1178	    let viewer = |value: &Expr| matches!(value, Expr::Name { name, .. } if name == "viewer");
  1179	    match rule {
  1180	        Expr::Name { name, .. } if name == "allow" => true,
  1181	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" =>
  1182	            model_read_by_form(left, pins) || model_read_by_form(right, pins),
  1183	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "and" =>
  1184	            model_read_by_form(left, pins) && model_read_by_form(right, pins),
  1185	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "=" => {
  1186	            let (column, value) = match (column_of(left), column_of(right)) {
  1187	                (Some(column), None) => (column, right.as_ref()),
  1188	                (None, Some(column)) => (column, left.as_ref()),
  1189	                _ => return false,
  1190	            };
  1191	            match (value, pinned(column)) {
  1192	                (value, Some(pin)) if viewer(value) => viewer(pin),
  1193	                (Expr::Literal { value, .. }, Some(Expr::Literal { value: pin, .. })) =>
  1194	                    value == pin && !matches!(value, Literal::Null),
  1195	                _ => false,
  1196	            }
  1197	        }
  1198	        _ => false,
  1199	    }
  1200	}

  1202	fn model_independent(expression: &Expr) -> bool {
  1203	    match expression {
  1204	        Expr::Literal { .. } | Expr::Name { .. } => true,
  1205	        Expr::Member { base, .. } | Expr::Unary { value: base, .. } => model_independent(base),
  1206	        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => model_independent(left) && model_independent(right),
  1207	        Expr::Call { args, .. } | Expr::Array { items: args, .. } => args.iter().all(model_independent),
  1208	        _ => false,
  1209	    }
  1210	}

  1212	fn model_equality_column(table: &Table, expression: &Expr) -> Option<String> {
  1213	    if let Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = expression && op == "=" {
  1214	        if let Some(column) = column_of(left) && model_independent(right) && table.col(column).is_some() { return Some(column.into()) }
  1215	        if let Some(column) = column_of(right) && model_independent(left) && table.col(column).is_some() { return Some(column.into()) }
  1216	    }
  1217	    let value = if let Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } = expression && op == "not" { value.as_ref() } else { expression };
  1218	    let column = column_of(value)?;
  1219	    matches!(table.col(column), Some(Kind::Bool)).then(|| column.into())
  1220	}
  1221	
  1222	fn model_pin_columns(table: &Table, expressions: &[&Expr], order: &[String]) -> Vec<String> {

  1225	}

  1227	fn model_lane_columns(mut pins: Vec<String>, order: &[String]) -> Vec<String> {
  1228	    for column in order {
  1229	        if !pins.contains(column) { pins.push(column.clone()); }
  1230	    }
  1231	    if !pins.iter().any(|column| column == "id") { pins.push("id".into()); }
  1232	    pins
  1233	}

  1235	fn party_column<'a>(table: &Table, expression: &'a Expr) -> Option<&'a str> {
  1236	    let Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = expression else { return None };
  1237	    if op != "=" { return None }
  1238	    let viewer = |value: &Expr| matches!(value, Expr::Name { name, .. } if name == "viewer");
  1239	    let column = if viewer(left) { column_of(right) } else if viewer(right) { column_of(left) } else { None }?;
  1240	    table.col(column).is_some().then_some(column)
  1241	}

  1243	fn membership_probe_kind(focal: &Table, expression: &Expr) -> Option<Kind> {

  1248	        Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } if op == "-"
  1249	            && matches!(value.as_ref(), Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Number(_), .. }) => Some(Kind::Int),
  1250	        _ => None,

  1252	}

     2	use super::catalog::Catalog;

     6	// identity_fits permits at most 135 bytes after JSON escaping; key escaping
     7	// is no larger. Include the key tag and two-byte terminator, even for byId
     8	// (the physical layout appends the identity a second time).
     9	const ID_BYTES: usize = 138;
    10	
    11	fn column_bytes(kind: &ColumnType) -> usize {
    12	    match kind {
    13	        ColumnType::Optional(inner) => column_bytes(inner),
    14	        ColumnType::Id | ColumnType::Principal | ColumnType::Ref { .. } => ID_BYTES,
    15	        ColumnType::Text { max } => (*max as usize).saturating_mul(4).saturating_add(3),
    16	        ColumnType::Bytes { max } => (*max as usize).saturating_mul(2).saturating_add(3),
    17	        ColumnType::Enum { variants } => variants.iter().map(|v| key::encode(&[Value::String(v.clone())]).len()).max().unwrap_or(1),
    18	        ColumnType::Bool => 1,
    19	        ColumnType::Int | ColumnType::IntRange { .. } | ColumnType::Decimal { .. } | ColumnType::Money { .. } | ColumnType::Time => 9,
    20	        // The existing schema validator refuses these in an index.
    21	        _ => usize::MAX,
    22	    }
    23	}

    25	/// Optional branch planning must not introduce a later key-bound refusal.
    26	/// Use the same column sizes and appended identity as the schema check.
    27	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#3 — oversized branch indexes leave the original read intact
    28	pub(super) fn plain_index_fits(table: &super::catalog::TableInfo, index: &crate::ast::IndexDecl, emitted_name: &str) -> bool {
    29	    table.decl.name.len().saturating_add(emitted_name.len()) <= MAX_INDEX_LABEL_BYTES
    30	        && index.words.is_none() && index.computed.is_empty()
    31	        && index.columns.iter().fold(ID_BYTES, |bytes, column| {
    32	            let size = table.columns.get(column).map(|ty| column_bytes(&crate::types::scalar_to_column(ty)))
    33	                .unwrap_or(if column == "id" { ID_BYTES } else { usize::MAX });
    34	            bytes.saturating_add(size)
    35	        }) <= MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES
    36	}

    38	// @ref LLP 3000.009#4.3 — bound components, encoding overhead and the id.
    39	pub(super) fn check(catalog: &Catalog, schema: &Schema, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
    40	    for (name, table) in &schema.tables {
    41	        for (index_name, index) in &table.indexes {
    42	            let site = catalog.site_for(&format!("tables.{name}.indexes.{index_name}"));
    43	            let generated = index_name.starts_with("$intent:");
    44	            let origin = if generated {
    45	                let unique = catalog.tables.get(name).is_some_and(|table| table.decl.indexes.iter().any(|index| index.name == *index_name && index.unique));
    46	                format!("{} at {site}", if unique { "unique declaration" } else { "access required by the declaration or read" })
    47	            } else { format!("index {name}.{index_name}") };
    48	            if name.len().saturating_add(index_name.len()) > MAX_INDEX_LABEL_BYTES {
    49	                let rewrite = if generated { "shorten the table or column names to leave space for the portable encoded key" } else { "shorten the table or index name to leave space for the portable encoded key" };
    50	                diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(&site, "E_INDEX_KEY_BYTES", format!("table and index labels for {origin} exceed {MAX_INDEX_LABEL_BYTES} combined UTF-8 bytes"), rewrite).guide("indexes"));
    51	            }
    52	            let mut components = Vec::new();
    53	            for component in &index.components {
    54	                match component {
    55	                    IndexComponent::Column(column) => {
    56	                        if let Some(kind) = table.columns.get(column) { components.push((column.clone(), column_bytes(kind))); }
    57	                    }
    58	                    IndexComponent::Calendar(key) => components.push((format!("{}({})", key.function, key.column), 9)),
    59	                    IndexComponent::WordPrefixes { .. } => components.push(("words(..)".into(), snapback4_core::words::MAX_TOKEN_BYTES + 3)),
    60	                    IndexComponent::Contribution(maintain) => {
    61	                        for column in &maintain.by {
    62	                            let bytes = if maintain.computed.contains_key(column) { 9 } else { table.columns.get(maintain.aliases.get(column).unwrap_or(column)).map_or(usize::MAX, column_bytes) };
    63	                            components.push((column.clone(), bytes));
    64	                        }
    65	                    }
    66	                }
    67	            }
    68	            let total = components.iter().fold(ID_BYTES, |n, (_, size)| n.saturating_add(*size));
    69	            if total <= MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES { continue; }
    70	            let (column, size) = components.iter().max_by_key(|(_, n)| n).cloned().unwrap_or(("id".into(), ID_BYTES));
    71	            let rest = total.saturating_sub(size);
    72	            let chars = MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES.saturating_sub(rest).saturating_sub(3) / 4;
    73	            let alternative = if generated { String::new() } else { format!(", or remove components from {index_name}") };
    74	            diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(&site, "E_INDEX_KEY_BYTES",
    75	                format!("{origin} can require {total} encoded bytes; column {name}.{column} exceeds the portable {MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES}-byte key budget including the id"),
    76	                format!("bound {column}, for example `{column}: text <={chars}`{alternative}; all components and the appended id must fit {MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES} bytes")).guide("indexes"));
    77	        }
    78	    }
    79	}

   546	pub(super) fn index_name(table: &TableInfo, index: &IndexDecl) -> String {
   547	    let mut components = index.columns.clone();
   548	    if components.last().map(String::as_str) == Some("id") && components.len() > 1 { components.pop(); }
   549	    if let Some((position, fields)) = &index.words { components.insert(*position, format!("words{}", fields.iter().map(|field| title(field)).collect::<String>())); }
   550	    let base = format!("by{}", components.iter().map(|column| {
   551	        if let Some(Expr::Call { name, args, .. }) = index.computed.get(column) {
   552	            format!("{}{}", title(name), args.first().map(crate::print::expression).map(|column| title(&column)).unwrap_or_default())
   553	        } else { title(column) }
   554	    }).collect::<String>());
   555	    let descriptor = (&index.columns, &index.words, computed_descriptor(table, &index.computed), index.unique);
   556	    let hash = snapback4_core::value::digest(&descriptor);
   557	    let mut name = format!("$intent:{base}:{}", &hash[..16]);
   558	    if table.indexes.iter().any(|index| index.name == name) { name = format!("$intent:{base}:{hash}"); }
   559	    name
   560	}
   561	
   562	fn title(value: &str) -> String { let mut chars = value.chars(); chars.next().map(|first| first.to_uppercase().collect::<String>() + chars.as_str()).unwrap_or_default() }
   563	
   564	pub(super) fn split(value: Expr, out: &mut Vec<Expr>) {
   565	    if let Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = &value && op == "and" { split(*left.clone(), out); split(*right.clone(), out); }
   566	    else { out.push(value); }
   567	}
   568	pub(super) fn conjunction(values: Vec<Expr>) -> Option<Box<Expr>> {
   569	    values.into_iter().reduce(|left, right| Expr::Binary { site: left.site().clone(), op: "and".into(), left: Box::new(left), right: Box::new(right) }).map(Box::new)
   570	}
   571	pub(super) fn column(value: &Expr) -> Option<&str> {
   572	    if let Expr::Member { base, name, .. } = value && matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Focal { .. }) { Some(name) } else { None }
   573	}
   574	pub(super) fn independent(value: &Expr) -> bool {
   575	    match value {
   576	        Expr::Literal { .. } | Expr::Name { .. } => true,
   577	        Expr::Member { base, .. } | Expr::Unary { value: base, .. } => independent(base),
   578	        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => independent(left) && independent(right),
   579	        Expr::Call { args, .. } | Expr::Array { items: args, .. } => args.iter().all(independent),
   580	        _ => false,
   581	    }
   582	}
   583	pub(super) fn equality(value: &Expr, table: &TableInfo) -> Option<(String, Expr)> {
   584	    if let Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = value && op == "=" {
   585	        if let Some(column) = column(left) && independent(right) { return Some((row_column(table, column).into(), *right.clone())); }
   586	        if let Some(column) = column(right) && independent(left) { return Some((row_column(table, column).into(), *left.clone())); }
   587	    }
   588	    let (value, boolean) = if let Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } = value && op == "not" { (value.as_ref(), false) } else { (value, true) };
   589	    let column = column(value)?;
   590	    (table.columns.get(column)?.kind == TypeKind::Bool).then(|| (column.into(), Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Bool(boolean), site: value.site().clone() }))
   591	}
   592	fn row_column<'a>(table: &TableInfo, column: &'a str) -> &'a str {
   593	    if column == "count" && !table.columns.contains_key(column)
   594	        && table.maintained.as_ref().is_some_and(|m| m.aggregate == "count") { "value" } else { column }
   595	}
   596	pub(super) fn comparison(value: &Expr) -> Option<(String, String, Expr)> {
   597	    let Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = value else { return None; };
   598	    if !matches!(op.as_str(), "<" | "<=" | ">" | ">=") { return None; }
   599	    if let Some(column) = column(left) && independent(right) { return Some((column.into(), op.clone(), *right.clone())); }
   600	    if let Some(column) = column(right) && independent(left) {
   601	        return Some((column.into(), match op.as_str() { "<" => ">", "<=" => ">=", ">" => "<", _ => "<=" }.into(), *left.clone()));
   602	    }
   603	    None
   604	}
   605	
   606	pub(super) fn computed_descriptor(table: &TableInfo, computed: &BTreeMap<String, Expr>) -> Vec<snapback4_core::maintain::CalendarKey> {
   607	    computed.iter().filter_map(|(name, value)| super::time_indexes::key(&table.decl.name, &table.columns, name, value).ok()).collect()
   608	}

   799	def parseCursor (order : List String) : Value → EM (Option Key)
   800	  | .s .null => pure none
   801	  | .arr xs =>

   809	def pointRead (vw : View) (t : String) (ks : List (String × Scalar)) : Value :=

   813	    | none => .null

   820	def cutScalar {α : Type} (cx : Ctx) : EM α :=
   821	  if cx.mutation then refuse "E_BOUND" else throw .unavailable

   556	
   557	/// Lazy lanes need a position that identifies one row within the lane.
   558	/// Keep setup evaluation and non-uniform traversal in their original order.
   559	pub fn lazy_merge(schema: &Schema, merge: &Merge) -> bool {
   560	    let Some(first) = merge.lanes.first() else { return true };
   561	    if merge.branches() { return branch_merge_lazy(schema, merge); }
   562	    merge.lanes.iter().all(|lane| {
   563	        let scan = &lane.scan;
   564	        lane.project.is_none() && lane.setup.is_empty() && scan.table == first.scan.table && scan.index == first.scan.index
   565	            && scan.prefix.iter().enumerate().all(|(position, key)| matches!(key, IndexKeyExpr::Scalar(_)) || position + 1 == scan.prefix.len())
   566	            && scan.order == first.scan.order && scan.prefix.len() == first.scan.prefix.len()
   567	            && scan_visibility(schema, scan).uniform
   568	            && schema.table(&scan.table).is_some_and(|table| table.index(&scan.index).is_some_and(|index| {
   569	                // Nullable unique keys allow multiple nulls; they are not a
   570	                // position from which a one-row refill can safely resume.
   571	                let total_unique = index.unique && index.components.iter().all(|component| component.key_type(table).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()));
   572	                scan.prefix.len() < index.components.len() && (total_unique || index.columns().last().is_some_and(|c| c == "id"))
   573	            }))
   574	    })
   575	}
   576	
   577	/// A branch merge's lanes may read different indexes of one table: each
   578	/// resumes on its own index from the shared merge position, so every lane
   579	/// needs scalar pins, a total position and the same position columns. A
   580	/// party or `where` lane must be decided by its prefix; a grant lane is
   581	/// decided per caller before it opens (`word_merge.rs`).
   582	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#3 — the binding check makes these hold; a lane that fails one keeps the eager traversal.
   583	pub fn branch_merge_lazy(schema: &Schema, merge: &Merge) -> bool {
   584	    let Some(first) = merge.lanes.first() else { return true };
   585	    let Some(table) = schema.table(&first.scan.table) else { return false };
   586	    let mut suffix: Option<(&crate::schema::Index, usize)> = None;
   587	    merge.lanes.iter().all(|lane| {
   588	        let scan = &lane.scan;
   589	        let Some(index) = table.index(&scan.index) else { return false };
   590	        let plain = index.components.iter().all(|component| component.column().is_some());
   591	        let total_unique = index.unique && plain && index.components.iter().all(|component| component.column()
   592	            .and_then(|column| table.columns.get(column)).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()));
   593	        let prefix_total_unique = table.indexes.values().filter(|candidate| candidate.unique && candidate.is_plain())
   594	            .filter(|candidate| candidate.components.iter().all(|component| component.column()
   595	                .and_then(|column| table.columns.get(column)).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional())))
   596	            .any(|candidate| candidate.components.iter().all(|part| part.column().is_some_and(|column|
   597	                index.components.iter().take(scan.prefix.len()).any(|prefix| prefix.column() == Some(column)))));
   598	        // A membership target selected by its intrinsic id is physically a
   599	        // fully pinned byId point, but its logical merge position is that id.
   600	        // Compare it as the unpinned id suffix used by every other lane.
   601	        let position_prefix = scan.prefix.len() - usize::from(membership_point_id(schema, lane));
   602	        let same = match suffix {
   603	            Some((first_index, first_prefix)) => same_merge_position(table, first_index, first_prefix, index, position_prefix),
   604	            None => { suffix = Some((index, position_prefix)); true }
   605	        };
   606	        let authority = if lane.membership() { membership_branch(schema, lane) }
   607	            else if lane.system() { lane.source.is_none() }
   608	            else if lane.vacancy() { vacancy_branch(schema, lane) }
   609	            else if lane.grant() { lane.source.is_none() && covering_grant(schema, scan).is_some() }
   610	            else if lane.guarded() { lane.source.is_none() && page_guard_decision(schema, scan).is_some() }
   611	            else { lane.source.is_none() && scan_visibility(schema, scan).uniform };
   612	        lane.project.is_none() && lane.setup.is_empty() && scan.table == first.scan.table
   613	            && scan.prefix.iter().all(|key| matches!(key, IndexKeyExpr::Scalar(_)))
   614	            && scan.order == first.scan.order && !scan.existence && same
   615	            && (scan.prefix.len() < index.components.len() || prefix_total_unique || membership_point_id(schema, lane))
   616	            && index.word_component().is_none()
   617	            // A dynamic target-id lane uses `[id, order…]`: its scalar id
   618	            // prefix is already a total point even though the physical index
   619	            // is non-unique and need not repeat id as a suffix.
   620	            && plain && (total_unique || prefix_total_unique
   621	                || index.components.last().and_then(|component| component.column()).is_some_and(|c| c == "id"))
   622	            && authority
   623	    })
   624	}
   625	
   626	/// Exhaustion is observable only when the authored read rule itself contains
   627	/// a top-level absence check on an exact required point keyed by this row's
   628	/// intrinsic id. The lane stays private and evaluates the complete rule.
   629	fn vacancy_branch(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
   630	    if lane.source.is_some() || lane.scan.filter.is_some() || !lane.scan.prefix.is_empty() { return false; }
   631	    let Some(outer) = schema.table(&lane.scan.table) else { return false };
   632	    if outer.sync.is_some() { return false; }
   633	    outer.rules.read.disjuncts().iter().any(|rule| {
   634	        let RuleExpr::Not(inner) = rule else { return false };
   635	        let RuleExpr::Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. } = inner.as_ref() else { return false };
   636	        let Some(target) = schema.table(table) else { return false };
   637	        let Some(point) = target.index(index) else { return false };
   638	        equalities.is_empty() && key == &[RuleTerm::Field("id".into())]
   639	            && point.unique && point.is_plain() && point.components.len() == 1
   640	            && point.components[0].column().is_some_and(|column|
   641	                target.columns.get(column).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()))
   642	    })
   643	}
   644	
   645	/// An intrinsic-id membership lane retains id as its logical merge position
   646	/// even though the physical byId scan pins that sole component.
   647	pub fn membership_point_id(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
   648	    if !lane.membership() || lane.scan.range.is_some() { return false; }
   649	    let Some(table) = schema.table(&lane.scan.table) else { return false };
   650	    let Some(index) = table.index(&lane.scan.index) else { return false };
   651	    let Some(Expr::Field { base, name: source_column }) = lane.distinct.as_ref() else { return false };
   652	    if !matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == &lane.binding) { return false; }
   653	    lane.scan.prefix.len() == index.components.len()
   654	        && index.components.last().and_then(|part| part.column()).is_some_and(|column| column == "id")
   655	        && membership_outer_column(schema, lane, source_column) == Some("id")
   656	}
   657	
   658	/// The source is readable under its fixed pins, and its row is the exact
   659	/// witness for the outer rule branch that the dynamic prefix opens. A cap on
   660	/// the fixed source group is one admission proof; a total point witness is
   661	/// another. The latter bounds each witness, while the shared read meter safely
   662	/// cuts the otherwise-unbounded inverted discovery before any row is emitted.
   663	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#15 — membership discovery proves each parent range without treating point uniqueness as a global membership cap.
   664	fn membership_branch(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
   665	    membership_witness(schema, lane, false)
   666	}
   667	
   668	/// A null discovered value may open a target lane only when the matching
   669	/// point witness identifies its row without that nullable component. A by-id
   670	/// probe with `parent = null` does; a nullable natural unique does not.
   671	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#14 — fixed-id witnesses preserve nullable residual equality without treating nullable natural keys as points.
   672	pub(crate) fn membership_null_distinct(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
   673	    membership_witness(schema, lane, true)
   674	}
   675	
   676	/// Whether PAGE v1's validator would admit this already-valid membership
   677	/// lane. Capability derivation uses the historical predicate exactly, so
   678	/// capped §14 plans remain v1-compatible while every newly admitted shape is
   679	/// fenced by `branch-membership-v2`.
   680	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#15 — PAGE2 capability negotiation preserves the v1 admission boundary.
   681	pub fn membership_v1(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
   682	    if !lane.membership() { return false; }
   683	    let Some(source) = lane.source.as_ref().and_then(crate::ir::LaneSource::scan) else { return false };
   684	    let Some(Expr::Field { base, name: source_column }) = lane.distinct.as_ref() else { return false };
   685	    if !matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(name) if name == &lane.binding) { return false; }
   686	    let Some(source_table) = schema.table(&source.table) else { return false };
   687	    let Some(source_index) = source_table.index(&source.index) else { return false };
   688	    let next_component = source.prefix.len() < source_index.components.len()
   689	        && source_index.components.get(source.prefix.len()).and_then(|part| part.column()) == Some(source_column);
   690	    let one_row_source = source.prefix.len() == source_index.components.len()
   691	        && source_index.unique && source_index.is_plain()
   692	        && source_index.components.iter().all(|part| part.column().and_then(|column| source_table.columns.get(column))
   693	            .is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()));
   694	    if source.existence || (!next_component && !one_row_source) || !scan_visibility(schema, source).decided_true { return false; }
   695	    let Bound::Literal(take @ 1..) = source.take else { return false };
   696	    let prefix_has = |field: &String| source_index.components.iter().take(source.prefix.len())
   697	        .any(|part| part.column() == Some(field));
   698	    let cap = source_table.constraints.iter().filter_map(|constraint| match constraint {
   699	        crate::schema::Constraint::Cap { by, max } if by.iter().all(prefix_has) && !source_table.cap_exempts_null(by) => Some(*max),
   700	        _ => None,
   701	    }).min();
   702	    if cap.is_none_or(|cap| cap > take && !one_row_source) { return false; }
   703	    membership_outer_column(schema, lane, source_column).is_some_and(|column| column != "id")
   704	}
   705	
   706	fn membership_outer_column<'a>(schema: &'a Schema, lane: &'a crate::ir::MergeLane, source_column: &str) -> Option<&'a str> {
   707	    let table = schema.table(&lane.scan.table)?;
   708	    let index = table.index(&lane.scan.index)?;
   709	    let mut outer = None;
   710	    for (part, key) in index.components.iter().zip(&lane.scan.prefix) {
   711	        let (Some(column), Some(expr)) = (part.column(), key.scalar()) else { return None };
   712	        if matches!(expr, Expr::Field { base, name } if matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == &lane.binding) && name == source_column)
   713	            && outer.replace(column).is_some() { return None; }
   714	    }
   715	    outer.map(String::as_str)
   716	}
   717	
   718	fn membership_witness(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane, null_distinct: bool) -> bool {
   719	    let Some(source) = lane.source.as_ref().and_then(crate::ir::LaneSource::scan) else { return false };
   720	    let Some(Expr::Field { base, name: source_column }) = lane.distinct.as_ref() else { return false };
   721	    if !matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(name) if name == &lane.binding) { return false; }
   722	    let Some(source_table) = schema.table(&source.table) else { return false };
   723	    let Some(source_index) = source_table.index(&source.index) else { return false };
   724	    let next_component = source.prefix.len() < source_index.components.len()
   725	        && source_index.components.get(source.prefix.len()).and_then(|part| part.column()) == Some(source_column);
   726	    // A fully pinned required unique identifies at most one source row, so
   727	    // any of that row's columns can be the discovered value. The source need
   728	    // not walk the distinct column as a following index component.
   729	    let one_row_source = source.prefix.len() == source_index.components.len()
   730	        && source_index.unique && source_index.is_plain()
   731	        && source_index.components.iter().all(|part| part.column().and_then(|column| source_table.columns.get(column))
   732	            .is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()));
   733	    if source.existence || (!next_component && !one_row_source)
   734	        || !scan_visibility(schema, source).decided_true { return false; }
   735	    // An applicable declaration can compile to a literal source bound. An
   736	    // exact total point may instead use the ordinary read meter: discovery
   737	    // remains fenced until that metered scan completes.
   738	    if !matches!(source.take, Bound::Meter | Bound::Literal(1..)) { return false; }
   739	    let prefix_has = |field: &String| source_index.components.iter().take(source.prefix.len())
   740	        .any(|part| part.column() == Some(field));
   741	    let capped_source_group = source_table.constraints.iter().any(|constraint| match constraint {
   742	        crate::schema::Constraint::Cap { by, max } if by.iter().all(prefix_has) && !source_table.cap_exempts_null(by) => Some(*max),
   743	        _ => None,
   744	    }.is_some());
   745	    let Some(outer_table) = schema.table(&lane.scan.table) else { return false };
   746	    let Some(outer_index) = outer_table.index(&lane.scan.index) else { return false };
   747	    let mut outer_column: Option<&String> = None;
   748	    for (part, key) in outer_index.components.iter().zip(&lane.scan.prefix) {
   749	        let (Some(column), Some(expr)) = (part.column(), key.scalar()) else { return false };
   750	        if matches!(expr, Expr::Field { base, name } if matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == &lane.binding) && name == source_column)
   751	            && outer_column.replace(column).is_some() { return false; }
   752	    }
   753	    let Some(outer_column) = outer_column else { return false };
   754	    let fixed = |column: &String| source_index.components.iter().zip(&source.prefix)
   755	        .find_map(|(part, key)| (part.column() == Some(column)).then(|| key.scalar()).flatten())
   756	        .or_else(|| source.filter.as_deref().and_then(|filter| required_source_equality(filter, column)));
   757	    let pins = scan_pinned(outer_table, &lane.scan);
   758	    let pinned = |column: &str| pins.iter().find_map(|(name, value)| (name.as_str() == column).then_some(*value));
   759	    membership_rule(&outer_table.rules.read, &|rule| {
   760	        let RuleExpr::Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. } = rule else { return false };
   761	        let Some(probe_index) = source_table.index(index) else { return false };
   762	        if table != &source.table || !probe_index.unique || !probe_index.is_plain() || probe_index.components.len() != key.len() { return false; }
   763	        let total_point = probe_index.components.iter().all(|part| part.column()
   764	            .and_then(|column| source_table.columns.get(column)).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()));
   765	        if !one_row_source && !capped_source_group && !total_point { return false; }
   766	        let matches = |column: &String, term: &RuleTerm| if column == source_column { matches!(term, RuleTerm::Field(field) if field == outer_column) }
   767	            else { fixed(column).is_some_and(|expr| match term {
   768	                // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#17 — an exact target pin fixes one component of a multi-column point witness.
   769	                RuleTerm::Field(field) => pinned(field).is_some_and(|pin| pin == expr),
   770	                _ => term_matches_expr(term, expr),
   771	            }) };
   772	        if !probe_index.components.iter().zip(key).all(|(part, term)| part.column().is_some_and(|column| matches(column, term)))
   773	            || !equalities.iter().all(|(column, term)| matches(column, term)) { return false; }
   774	        !null_distinct || probe_index.components.iter().all(|part| part.column().is_some_and(|column| column != source_column
   775	            && fixed(column).is_some_and(|expr| matches!(expr, Expr::Input(Input::Viewer)) || fixed_non_null_literal(expr))))
   776	    }, &|rule| rule_true(rule, &lane.scan.table, outer_table, &pinned)
   777	        || lane.scan.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(|filter|
   778	            fixed_rule_holds(schema, filter, rule, &lane.scan.table, outer_table, &pinned, &pins)))
   779	}
   780	
   781	/// Every sibling of a varying membership witness must already hold. Preserve
   782	/// authored Boolean grouping: every fixed conjunct is required, while proving
   783	/// one fixed alternative is enough for an authored disjunction.
   784	fn fixed_rule_holds<'a>(schema: &Schema, filter: &Expr, rule: &RuleExpr,
   785	    outer_name: &str, outer_table: &Table, pinned: &impl Fn(&str) -> Option<&'a Expr>,
   786	    pins: &[(&String, &Expr)]) -> bool {
   787	    if rule_true(rule, outer_name, outer_table, pinned) { return true; }
   788	    match rule {
   789	        RuleExpr::And(parts) => parts.iter().all(|part|
   790	            fixed_rule_holds(schema, filter, part, outer_name, outer_table, pinned, pins)),
   791	        RuleExpr::Or(parts) => parts.iter().any(|part|
   792	            fixed_rule_holds(schema, filter, part, outer_name, outer_table, pinned, pins)),
   793	        _ => fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, filter, rule, pins),
   794	    }
   795	}
   796	
   797	/// A generated membership lane may retain prefix-fixed point grants
   798	/// as a residual predicate.  Recognize only the exact bound point expression:
   799	/// this proves that every row emitted by the lane satisfies that conjunct,
   800	/// while a missing, reordered or merely similar probe keeps the merge out.
   801	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — fixed grants may guard one varying per-row grant.
   802	fn fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema: &Schema, filter: &Expr, rule: &RuleExpr,
   803	    pins: &[(&String, &Expr)]) -> bool {
   804	    if let Expr::Binary { op: crate::ir::BinaryOp::And, left, right } = filter {
   805	        return fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, left, rule, pins)
   806	            || fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, right, rule, pins);
   807	    }
   808	    // The compiler lowers a left-associated `a and b and c` into successive
   809	    // Let/If pairs. Accept only that exact dataflow chain: every right operand
   810	    // assigns the current result on its true branch, while `or` assigns on the
   811	    // false branch and any extra statement or mismatched name is rejected.
   812	    if let Expr::Block { body, value } = filter {
   813	        if body.is_empty() || body.len() % 2 != 0 { return false; }
   814	        let mut previous: Option<&String> = None;
   815	        let mut matched = false;
   816	        let mut at = 0;
   817	        while at < body.len() {
   818	            let [crate::ir::Stmt::Let { name, value: left }, crate::ir::Stmt::If {
   819	                condition: Expr::Variable(condition), then_body, else_body }] = &body[at..at + 2] else { return false };
   820	            let [crate::ir::Stmt::Assign { name: assigned, value: right }] = then_body.as_slice() else { return false };
   821	            if !else_body.is_empty() || condition != name || assigned != name { return false; }
   822	            if let Some(previous) = previous {
   823	                if !matches!(left, Expr::Variable(input) if input == previous) { return false; }
   824	            } else {
   825	                matched |= fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, left, rule, pins);
   826	            }
   827	            matched |= fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, right, rule, pins);
   828	            previous = Some(name);
   829	            at += 2;
   830	        }
   831	        if !matches!((value.as_ref(), previous), (Expr::Variable(result), Some(previous)) if result == previous) { return false; }
   832	        return matched;
   833	    }
   834	    let (Expr::Exists(read), RuleExpr::Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. }) = (filter, rule) else { return false };
   835	    let Some(target) = schema.table(table) else { return false };
   836	    let Some(point) = target.index(index) else { return false };
   837	    equalities.is_empty() && target.through.is_none() && target.not_before.is_none() && !target.is_expiring()
   838	        && point.unique && point.is_plain() && point.components.iter().all(|part| part.column()
   839	            .and_then(|column| target.columns.get(column)).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()))
   840	        && &read.table == table && &read.index == index && read.key.len() == key.len()
   841	        && read.key.iter().zip(key).all(|(expression, term)| rule_term_expression(term, expression, pins))
   842	        && point_read_is_visible(schema, read)
   843	}
   844	
   845	/// A rule probe uses system authority, but the retained residual point uses
   846	/// ordinary caller authority. Re-prove that the generated, pin-substituted
   847	/// point is visible instead of asking about the unsubstituted authored terms.
   848	fn point_read_is_visible(schema: &Schema, read: &crate::ir::Read) -> bool {
   849	    let Some(table) = schema.table(&read.table) else { return false };
   850	    let Some(index) = table.index(&read.index) else { return false };
   851	    if !index.is_plain() || index.components.len() != read.key.len() { return false; }
   852	    let pinned = |name: &str| index.components.iter().zip(&read.key).find_map(|(part, value)|
   853	        (part.column().is_some_and(|column| column == name)).then_some(value));
   854	    visibility(&read.table, table, &table.rules.read, &pinned).decided_true
   855	}
   856	
   857	fn rule_term_expression(term: &RuleTerm, expression: &Expr, pins: &[(&String, &Expr)]) -> bool {
   858	    match (term, expression) {
   859	        (RuleTerm::Viewer, Expr::Input(Input::Viewer)) => true,
   860	        (RuleTerm::Literal(value), Expr::Literal(actual)) => value == actual,
   861	        (RuleTerm::Field(field), expression) => pins.iter().find_map(|(name, value)|
   862	            (name.as_str() == field).then_some(*value)).is_some_and(|value| value == expression),
   863	        _ => false,
   864	    }
   865	}
   866	
   867	/// A witness supplies one conjunct; every other conjunct must already hold
   868	/// by the lane's pins. Distribution does not drop a status/role condition.
   869	fn membership_rule(rule: &RuleExpr, witness: &impl Fn(&RuleExpr) -> bool, pinned: &impl Fn(&RuleExpr) -> bool) -> bool {
   870	    match rule {
   871	        RuleExpr::Or(parts) => parts.iter().any(|part| membership_rule(part, witness, pinned)),
   872	        RuleExpr::And(parts) => parts.iter().enumerate().any(|(at, part)|
   873	            membership_rule(part, witness, pinned) && parts.iter().enumerate().all(|(other, rule)| at == other || pinned(rule))),
   874	        _ => witness(rule),
   875	    }
   876	}
   877	
   878	/// A generated membership discovery filter may retain an exact fixed probe
   879	/// component after its capped prefix (`id = 'm1'`, for example). Only a bare
   880	/// equality in the filter's conjunction is a necessary fact about every
   881	/// discovered row; alternatives and other implications remain unsupported.
   882	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#14 — fixed membership witness columns may be proved by exact source residuals.
   883	fn required_source_equality<'a>(filter: &'a Expr, column: &str) -> Option<&'a Expr> {
   884	    match filter {
   885	        Expr::Binary { op: crate::ir::BinaryOp::And, left, right } =>
   886	            required_source_equality(left, column).or_else(|| required_source_equality(right, column)),
   887	        Expr::Binary { op: crate::ir::BinaryOp::Eq, left, right } if source_field(left, column) => Some(right),
   888	        Expr::Binary { op: crate::ir::BinaryOp::Eq, left, right } if source_field(right, column) => Some(left),
   889	        _ => None,
   890	    }
   891	}
   892	
   893	fn source_field(expr: &Expr, column: &str) -> bool {
   894	    matches!(expr, Expr::Field { base, name }
   895	        if name == column && matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == "$filter"))
   896	}
   897	
   898	fn term_matches_expr(term: &RuleTerm, expr: &Expr) -> bool {
   899	    match (term, expr) { (RuleTerm::Viewer, Expr::Input(Input::Viewer)) => true,
   900	        (RuleTerm::Literal(value), expr) => literal_matches_expr(value, expr), _ => false }

   870	            let scope_rewrite = existing.map(|(name, _)| format!("pin {} with where equalities as well as the current equality prefix, choosing the intended scope (this narrows the answer); the existing index `{name}: {columns}` supports it; or read the page on the device", unpinned.iter().map(|column| format!(".{column}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")));
   871	            let equality_index_rewrite = equality_filter_index_rewrite(catalog, table, scan, &unpinned, &leading,

   873	            let cap_repair = bound
   874	                .sources
   875	                .get(&scan.site)

   878	            // @ref LLP 3000.036#18 — a runtime parent guard can certify the page for its holders.

   880	                let mut fields = std::collections::BTreeSet::new(); guard.fields(&mut fields); !fields.is_empty()
   881	            });
   882	            notes.push(
   883	                Diagnostic::new(
   884	                    &bound.site,
   885	                    if conditional { "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE" } else { "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" },
   886	                    if held_rewrite.is_some() { format!("query {}: this lookup does not pin an authorized row's id; matching a name does not prove uniqueness or absence. The server may return matches but cannot certify complete. The held replacement (check --verbose) keeps the same matches and shape on the device; it requires acquired coverage and has no server fallback. Keep the original query if server lookup is required", bound.program.name) }
   887	                    else {

   889	                        // @ref LLP 3000.036#13.2 — the one line says what a test and a client do with the verdict, and where the complete form is.
